【上海“星樞計劃”首發星座釋出】7月18日，在2026世界人工智慧大會上，上海標誌性太空數字基建工程“星樞計劃”首發星座正式對外發布。儀式由WAIC組委會與復旦大學聯合主辦，作為上海重點佈局的太空算力產業標杆專案，專案定位為上海市旗艦級太空算力星座工程，該星座由三顆衛星組成，核心是透過太空直接處理資料，減少對地面裝置的依賴。根據規劃，該專案將分階段實施：驗證階段發射2顆算力星和12顆邊緣計算星；商業部署階段擴充套件至50顆算力星和100顆邊緣計算星；最終完成千星部署。未來將為氣象、應急、自然資源等領域提供太空算力和遙感服務，預計五年帶動上下游企業超百家，相關產業年產值有望突破千億元。該星座還將向共建“一帶一路”國家提供氣象和遙感算力服務，支援跨境災害監測。(央視新聞)

【7月美元存款利率生變，外資銀行限時加息】7月以來，部分外資銀行推出了限時高息美元定期存款活動，普遍上調各期限美元存款利率，部分產品年化利率達到4%。與之形成對比的是，國有大行、股份行、城商行的美元存款利率近期未發生調整。國家金融與發展實驗室副主任曾剛表示，近期部分銀行上調美元定期存款利率，其核心背景是7月底美聯儲議息會議臨近，市場對短期內繼續維持利率不變有一定預期，加之部分銀行有攬儲和調節外匯資金來源的需求，便透過上調美元利率吸引客戶存入外幣，緩解自身美元流動性壓力。曾剛提示，若投資者近期有明確的美元使用場景，可按需配置美元存款，減少額外匯率波動帶來的虧損風險。另外，投資者應嚴格控制配置比例與產品久期，美元資產佔個人總資產比例應控制在合理範圍內，並優先選擇3至6個月的短期美元存款，避免長期鎖定資金，以靈活應對匯率、利率變動。(中證金牛座)

### 2026-07-19 09:01:02  #市場

【飛天茅臺大漲 白酒大單品總價創五天新高】新浪財經“酒價內參”過去24小時收集的資料顯示，中國白酒市場主要大單品的終端零售均價7月19日顯著反彈。如果主要單品各取一瓶整體打包售賣，今日總售價為9840元，較昨日大幅飆升35元，結束此前連續七天回落頹勢，並創下五天以來最高。飛天茅臺零售均價在i茅臺平臺上調售價後快速跟漲，單日上漲35元/瓶，漲幅與今日白酒大單品總價的增量相同，成為今日總價反彈的核心驅動力。縱觀全場，核心單品的漲跌分化依然顯著，終端交投情緒環比回暖，總價自階段低點明顯修復，但低位震盪格局尚未完全扭轉。今日11大白酒單品五漲五跌一平，漲跌家數均衡，但贏家漲幅大幅勝出。上漲方面，飛天茅臺漲幅居前，大幅上漲35元/瓶，創近一月最大單日漲幅，價格升至1753元/瓶並創近一月最高價紀錄；按照i茅臺最新官方定價1639元/瓶計，其全國終端零售均價相對官方定價的價差擴大至114元/瓶。古井貢古20上漲8元/瓶，重新站上530元關口；五糧液普五八代上漲6元/瓶，連續兩日自“酒價內參”去年11月有統計以來最低位處修復，但仍處於790元關口下方；國窖1573上漲4元/瓶，回補昨日全部跌幅；習酒君品漲2元/瓶，再度逼近630元關口。下跌方面，五糧液1618跌幅居前，下跌6元/瓶，平近一月最大單日跌幅，價格降至813元/瓶並創近一月最低價紀錄，近一月其價格中樞持續下移；青花汾20與洋河夢之藍M6+均下跌5元/瓶，其中前者回吐此前兩日全部漲幅，再度跌破370元關口，後者同樣盡失此前兩日全部戰果，再平近一月最低價紀錄；精品茅臺下跌3元/瓶；青花郎小跌1元/瓶。水晶劍南春價格環比昨日保持不變，連續三日維持在403元/瓶。

### 2026-07-19 07:24:52  #其他

【四部門印發通知部署做好地質災害風險隱患巡排查工作】
記者18日從應急管理部獲悉，國家防災減災救災委員會辦公室、自然資源部、水利部、應急管理部17日印發緊急通知，部署各地區、各有關部門和單位切實做好地質災害風險隱患巡查排查和災害防範應對等工作。

通知指出，近日，甘肅宕昌縣、重慶彭水縣接連發生山體滑坡、崩塌造成重大人員傷亡，暴露出地質災害風險隱患常態化巡查排查覆蓋不全面、對災害前兆發現研判不及時等問題。

通知強調，要做好重要隱患點巡查排查，排查穩定性差、近期活動跡象明顯、近兩年監測預警裝置出現過紅色預警、切坡建房擾動、降雨為主要影響因素的在冊地質災害點。排查複核災害點（含近5年已銷號點）基本特徵、威脅物件、危險區範圍、已安裝監測裝置執行情況和已採取的防治措施及有效性。要做好重點風險區巡查排查，對轄區內已確定的中等及以上等級風險區，特別是位於城鎮、村組等重點部位的風險區，要逐一排查，要對陡坡陡崖、切坡建房等開展重點複核，對出現變形跡象的要立即納入隱患臺賬，安排監測預警等防範措施。

通知要求，針對前期降雨量大、地下水豐富、溼度變化大、旱澇急轉、人類工程活動強烈的地質災害風險區要加強防範手段，及時研判地質災害發生發展趨勢，及時透過多渠道、多形式釋出預警預報資訊。要落實好臨災預警“叫醒”“叫應”機制，果斷轉移受威脅群眾，對於老弱病殘孕幼等特殊群體落實幫扶措施，切實做到“應轉早轉”“應轉盡轉”，不落一戶一人。（新華社）

【伊拉克宣佈將重修跨國輸油管道】當地時間18日，伊拉克石油部宣佈，伊拉克和敘利亞簽署了一份諒解備忘錄，將重修一條從伊拉克通往敘利亞地中海沿岸的原油出口管道。據瞭解，這條輸油管道原建於1952年，東起伊拉克基爾庫克，西至敘利亞巴尼亞斯，全長約800公里。這條管道在兩伊戰爭和伊拉克戰爭中受到嚴重損壞，長期被棄用。伊拉克石油部表示，該專案有助於實現伊拉克原油出口路線多樣化，加強能源基礎設施建設與地區能源安全，為伊拉克原油通往全球市場創造一條新的戰略走廊。由於近期霍爾木茲海峽受阻，伊拉克原油出口大為受限。伊朗戰事爆發前，伊拉克95%的原油經霍爾木茲海峽運出，政府財政90%依賴石油收入。據英國沃泰克薩諮詢公司統計，伊拉克海運石油出口量在今年5月一度暴跌至去年同期的8%。為打通出口通道，伊拉克把目光投向這條年久失修的管道。但有分析認為，由於管道長期被棄用，需要全面維修，修好可能要花兩三年時間。（央視新聞）

【民調：近半數美國人認為最高法院裁決受政治立場左右】華盛頓郵報與益普索聯合民調顯示，超半數美國人不認可聯邦最高法院的工作表現；46%的受訪者認為，法院在審理特朗普政府相關政策案件時，依據的是意識形態而非法律條文。本月出爐的這份支援率資料，創下該院近年新低，也反映出一種長期趨勢：越來越多美國人認為法院判決帶有黨派偏向。在今年6月落幕的司法年度裡，最高法院多項裁決為特朗普政府帶來關鍵勝利——擴大總統行政權力、允許政府驅逐數十萬在美合法居留移民。但在其他核心政策議題上，法院也曾駁回特朗普的訴求：例如維持落地出生公民權、叫停特朗普對全球多國商品加徵大範圍關稅的計劃；法院還裁定，至少現階段總統無權解僱美聯儲董事會內的民主黨籍委員。這份新民調顯示，55%的美國人對最高法院履職持負面評價，僅41%表示認可。該數值徘徊在歷史低位，但尚未跌至2022年的不滿峰值。當年蓋洛普民調資料顯示，58%民眾不認可法院工作，僅40%持正面態度。

【加拿大山火持續 美多地釋出空氣質量、強風暴、洪水等預警】受加拿大山火的濃煙影響，截至當地時間7月18日早晨，從美國上中西部、五大湖地區一直延伸到新英格蘭地區，再向南覆蓋東北部和中大西洋地區的十餘州已釋出空氣質量警報。據悉，目前加拿大有900多處山火仍在燃燒，大量煙霧持續飄向美國東北部和中大西洋地區。不過，一個將在18日經過的風暴系統有望幫助驅散部分煙霾。與此同時，在西北風的推動下，一股濃煙帶正進入美國中西部和五大湖上游地區，預計當地空氣質量將在整個週末維持較差水平。另一方面，美國風暴預測中心已對俄亥俄河谷、東北部和中大西洋部分地區釋出三級（最高五級）強對流天氣風險預警。95號州際公路沿線的紐約、費城、巴爾的摩和華盛頓特區等主要城市都可能受到影響，匹茲堡、克利夫蘭和布法羅也預計將遭遇強雷暴。多輪降雨和雷暴將帶來破壞性大風、冰雹、少量龍捲風，部分地區有山洪暴發的風險。紐約市、費城等美國東北部部分地區已釋出洪水監測預警，有效期持續至18日晚間。

【Turing Quantum launches industry-grade quantum-classical hybrid agent platform QAgent】July 18 — Turing Quantum unveiled QAgent at the 2026 World Artificial Intelligence Conference (WAIC). The company says QAgent is the world’s first photonic-quantum-based, industry-deployable quantum-classical hybrid agent platform, exposing large language models and agents as a single scheduling interface. Built on Turing Quantum’s proprietary photonic quantum computing stack, QAgent integrates a hybrid quantum-classical compute layer, the DeepQuantum quantum AI programming framework, a quantum algorithm library and industry toolkits, and supports an end-to-end workflow from natural-language input and task decomposition to coordinated quantum-agent invocation, quantum compute scheduling and result aggregation. Turing positions the product as a step toward moving quantum computing from frontier research capacity to industry capability and agent-driven commercial services.

【院士談中國算力網發展：第一階段算力匯聚已完成70%】7月17-20日，2026世界人工智慧大會暨人工智慧全球治理高階別會議（WAIC）在上海舉行。鵬城實驗室主任、中國工程院院士高文在大會“未來計算·未來算力”論壇上談及中國算力網發展的三個階段。高文表示，第一階段是算力資源匯聚，目前該階段任務已完成70%，全國70%的算力資源已納入實時更新的統一資料庫，該資料庫提供給國家資料局使用，可實時展示各算力中心的執行狀態等。第二階段可能是在算力匯聚的基礎上，面向特定使用者群體開展協同運算，如在部分廠商內部或友商之間，暫不對普通公眾開放，這一階段將持續數年。待第二階段的運營模式成熟，配套的計費、管理到位後，算力網才會面向社會開放，進入第三階段。高文指出，第一階段已完成70%，第二階段預計還需五年左右推進，而第三階段的全面落地並非三五年即可實現，可能需要十幾年、二十年的時間。（21世紀經濟報道）

【陳吉寧會見圖靈獎得主、Openmind研究院首席科學家理查德·薩頓】上海市委書記陳吉寧今天會見了來滬參加2026世界人工智慧大會暨人工智慧全球治理高階別會議的圖靈獎得主、Openmind研究院首席科學家理查德·薩頓一行。陳吉寧介紹了上海人工智慧發展相關情況。他說，習近平主席在2026世界人工智慧大會暨人工智慧全球治理高階別會議開幕式上發表主旨講話，就攜手構建公正合理的全球人工智慧治理體系提出4點意見，為我們更加健康、安全、有序發展人工智慧指明瞭方向。人工智慧是上海重點發展的三大先導產業之一，我們秉持以人為本、向上向善理念，保持戰略敏捷、堅持久久為功，著力加強基礎研究，培育最佳化創新生態，加快建設人工智慧發展和治理創新高地。薩頓先生是人工智慧領域知名學者，歡迎抓住難得的歷史性機遇，把自身研究優勢同上海的創新優勢、人才優勢、生態優勢等更加緊密結合起來，在加強基礎研究、人才交流培養等方面深化溝通對接和開放合作，讓人工智慧這一前沿技術更好造福人類社會。上海將持續營造開放包容的創新生態，提供高效便利的對接服務，更好支援全球科學家和各路人才在上海這座城市創新創業、成就夢想。理查德·薩頓表示，人工智慧建立在人類文明發展、人類持續學習基礎上，開源開放、合作共享、互利共贏是推動技術發展的重要前提。此次上海之行同各方面研究人員和研發機構開展了深度交流，充分感受到上海這座城市發展人工智慧的創新活力與優良生態。將秉持“理解智慧並與世界分享”宗旨，深化學術交流，加強經驗互鑑，促進國際合作，攜手探索人工智慧教育創新與基礎研究發展。 (上觀新聞)

### 2026-07-18 16:36:00  #公司

【騰訊首次升級釋出具身智慧全棧佈局】7月18日，在2026世界人工智慧大會（WAIC）上，騰訊正式升級釋出具身智慧全棧方案，該方案貫穿雲底座、模型層、平臺層與應用層，全面助力機器人本體及系統開發商提質提效。會上，騰訊首次系統性展示了具身智慧全棧佈局，實現從基座模型、原生智慧體到開發平臺的全面躍升。在模型層，騰訊持續投入基礎研發，構建起涵蓋Hy-Embodied-VLA-0.5、Hy-Embodied-VLM-1.0與Hy-Embodied-RxBrain-1.0的具身模型矩陣。目前，上述模型已被應用於真實工業場景，並在導購導覽、養老服務等多個任務中完成落地驗證，藉助軟硬一體的快速適配能力，同一套模型可支撐多形態機器人規模化應用。具身智慧體方面，騰訊同步釋出TairosAgent具身智慧體框架與Apexio智慧體，可將“左右腦”“大腦”“小腦”和身體整合成完整的系統，讓機器人形成一個持續感知、持續決策、持續行動的整體。於2025年釋出的鈦螺絲（Tairos）平臺也迎來升級，將為行業提供模型、智慧體與開發工具等一系列開源能力與服務，降低從模型到本體再到應用全鏈條的開發門檻。此外，在基礎設施能力上，騰訊雲圍繞具身智慧搭建從算力底座、模型服務到感知互動的三層體系，打造覆蓋研發、訓練與交付的具身智慧雲+AI底座。騰訊雲還攜手行業夥伴，打造業內首個雲端EaaS（Embodied-AI-as-a-Service）具身智慧服務，助力加速具身智慧從技術創新走向規模化應用。（上證報）

【山西省退役軍人事務廳原一級巡視員範波濤被開除黨籍】中央紀委國家監委網站7月18日通報，據山西省紀委監委訊息：日前，經山西省委批准，山西省紀委監委對山西省退役軍人事務廳原一級巡視員範波濤嚴重違紀違法問題進行了立案審查調查。經查，範波濤背離初心使命，喪失黨性原則，無視中央八項規定精神，收受禮品、禮金，接受可能影響公正執行公務的旅遊安排；違反組織原則，在組織談話函詢時不如實說明問題，在職務調整方面為他人謀取利益並收受財物；不正確履職，推動有關景區綜合整改整治工作不力，造成不良影響；利用職務上的便利以及職權或地位形成的便利條件，為他人在工程承攬、企業經營等方面謀利，並非法收受鉅額財物。範波濤嚴重違反黨的組織紀律、廉潔紀律、工作紀律，構成嚴重職務違法並涉嫌受賄犯罪，且在黨的十八大後不收斂、不收手，性質嚴重，影響惡劣，應予嚴肅處理。依據《中國共產黨紀律處分條例》《中華人民共和國監察法》《中華人民共和國公職人員政務處分法》等有關規定，經山西省紀委常委會會議研究並報山西省委批准，決定給予範波濤開除黨籍處分；按規定取消其享受的待遇；收繳其違紀違法所得；將其涉嫌犯罪問題移送檢察機關依法審查起訴，所涉財物一併移送。

【張國清趕赴重慶彭水縣指導山體崩塌災害現場搜救和應急處置工作時強調 全力科學搜救 嚴防次生災害 細緻做好受災群眾安置和基本生活保障】根據習近平總書記重要指示和李強總理要求，中共中央政治局委員、國務院副總理張國清代表黨中央、國務院，17日晚率有關部門負責同志趕赴重慶彭水縣指導山體崩塌災害現場搜救和應急處置工作。張國清抵達後立即前往山體崩塌災害現場，詳細瞭解救援進展和困難。18日早晨，張國清再次來到災害現場研究最佳化救援方案，併到縣人民醫院、臨時安置點看望受傷人員和轉移安置群眾，轉達習近平總書記的關懷牽掛。隨後主持召開會議，傳達習近平總書記重要指示批示精神和李強總理批示、視訊通話要求，研究部署支援幫助重慶搶險救援工作。張國清強調，要千方百計搜救失聯人員，針對災害現場救援作業空間受限、次生災害風險大、連續降雨等情況，強化現場指揮和安全管控，組織專業救援力量和適用搶險救援裝備開展搜救。周圍山體仍存在坍塌風險，要強化邊坡和殘留滑坡體實時監測預警與救援聯動，嚴防救援過程中發生次生災害。要竭盡全力救治受傷群眾，用心用情細緻做好遇難和失聯人員家屬安撫工作，加強心理疏導，保障好轉移群眾基本生活。他強調，近期防災減災救災形勢嚴峻，極端天氣多發頻發，各地區各有關方面要樹牢底線思維、極限思維，進一步壓緊壓實隱患排查、預警叫應、轉移避險、救援救助等各環節責任措施。要緊盯臨山臨江、切坡建房等高風險點位，加強專業巡查排險，充分發揮基層網格員作用，發現險情堅決果斷轉移，切實保障人民群眾生命財產安全。（新華社）

【Iran proposes environmental service fee for ships transiting Strait of Hormuz】Fars News reports Iran’s Department of Environment international affairs director has submitted implementation rules to government to levy an environmental service fee on vessels transiting the Strait of Hormuz. Fees would be set according to vessel type, cargo, transit record and assessed environmental-risk level. Officials cite UNCLOS and related treaties as legal basis, saying coastal states may intervene and charge for services if innocent passage is breached or if national security, public health or the environment are threatened; chargeable services are described as public-health and environmental protection measures. The draft does not specify payment mechanisms; detailed execution will be determined by the competent authorities, with proceeds intended to fund navigational safety and sustainable transit in the Strait.

【FCA's new aggregated short-position disclosures contain errors, raising market doubts】The Financial Conduct Authority this week published short-position data under its new regime, which replaces name-level disclosure of significant short holders with aggregated per-stock totals, the Financial Times reports. Data provider Breakout Point identified multiple clear errors: positions later deleted or altered with no visible amendment record, and some entries that appear to be years-old and unlikely to still exist. 【Scotiabank: ECB likely to hold rates in July amid elevated uncertainty】Scotiabank expects the ECB to hold rates in July, arguing policy need not move in a straight line during periods of high uncertainty. Markets have priced a 25bp June hike and expect the deposit rate to be left at 2.25% thereafter, with consensus leaning to stand pat in July. ECB President Lagarde said on July 1: "I think the upside risks to inflation and downside risks to growth may be more balanced than a few weeks ago, because things are changing fast." Since then US‑Iran tensions have flared and WTI and Brent have each risen roughly $12/barrel. June euro‑area CPI fell 0.1% MoM and core CPI eased YoY to 2.4% from 2.6%, which may give the ECB more time to assess under its data‑dependent framework; market sentiment indicators nonetheless remain overall tilted hawkish.

【中國足協釋出有關申思等“禁足”人員的情況通報】中國足協18日釋出有關申思等“禁足”人員的情況通報，原文如下:

近日，中國足球協會關注到有關申思、祁宏參與足球青訓活動相關事件的資訊，現通報如下情況：

1.《關於對原上海中遠匯麗足球俱樂部運動員申思等四人違規違紀行為的處罰決定》（足紀字〔2013〕028號）已註明，中國足球協會終身禁止申思、祁宏從事任何與足球有關的活動。

2.《中國足球協會紀律準則》第二十四條針對“禁止從事任何與足球有關的活動”作明確解釋：禁止從事任何與足球有關的活動是指禁止被處罰物件從事任何與足球有關的活動（包括但不限於投資、管理、比賽或其他）。

3.中國足球協會將密切關注上海市有關部門對事件的調查進展，調查結果將及時向社會通報。

### 2026-07-18 12:11:25  #行業

【崔東樹：鋰電池消費稅調整，是對整車企業造電池的有力助推，有利於中國汽車產業做大做強】乘聯分會秘書長崔東樹認為：鋰電池消費稅調整，是對整車企業造電池的有力助推，有利於中國汽車產業做大做強。我對此的核心判斷是：這次政策調整意味著“油電同權”的大幕正式拉開。從購置稅減半、車船稅免徵取消，到電池消費稅恢復徵收，新能源汽車的稅收優惠“保護期”正在分階段、有節奏地退出。燃油車長期承擔全額車船稅與燃油稅，插混、增程式及新能源商用車卻享受免稅紅利，形成“有排放、用道路、獲收益卻不承擔稅負”的漏洞。油電同權不是“一刀切”均等化，而是依據技術屬性、排放特徵、使用場景，建立權責匹配、稅負公平的汽車稅制體系。

### 2026-07-18 12:10:43  #MKT News #Market Themes #Magnificent 7 #Chip Wars #S&P500 #Impact bearish #Nasdaq100 #Nikkei 225

【Unresolved doubts driving the equity sell-off】Markets are increasingly doubting prior certainties, a dynamic amplifying the current equity decline. The S&P 500 fell 1.6% this week, the Nasdaq Composite dropped 2.9% and the Nasdaq-100 lost 2.7%, putting July on track to be the worst in over 20 years. The Philadelphia Semiconductor Index is down about 20% from its June 22 peak and entered bear-market territory on Friday. Asian pain is visible: KOSPI is off more than 25% and Japan’s Nikkei 225 plunged 4% on Friday, entering a technical correction. The sell-off lacks a single clear catalyst; cited drivers include concerns that chip-sector gains in 1H ran too far, large tech borrowing to fund capex plans, and persistent macro uncertainty under new Fed policy. BTIG chief market technical analyst Jonathan Krinsky warned a summer-style correction similar to 2024 could push the S&P 500 below its 200-day moving average at 6,983 points. If that occurs, semiconductors would likely weaken further and recent breadth trades and the Magnificent Seven could stall and turn down. Market action appears to be moving in that direction despite the absence of an obviously dangerous headline.

【聯想集團副總裁陳敏儀：AI不應是少數人的奢侈品，而應該成為像水和電一樣的基礎設施】聯想集團副總裁、SSG業務應用服務交付部負責人陳敏儀日前在上海舉辦的WAIC“共贏金磚”論壇上指出，AI的終極價值不是技術本身的高度，而是技術惠及世界的廣度。AI不應是少數人的奢侈品，而應該成為像水和電一樣的基礎設施。她稱，當前人工智慧技術成為推動產業升級和社會經濟發展的核心生產力。依託自研技術，聯想為本屆世界盃量身定製了多項AI技術方案，這些技術應用不僅推動AI重塑體育行業，更實踐和驗證了AI在技術平權、消費體驗提質和全要素管理等政策導向上的真實價值，並且探索出一條AI普惠的新正規化：從頂級場景驗證，到標準化方案沉澱，再到規模化複製推廣，這是聯想推動AI普惠的核心方法論。

【Assessment: Iran escalates strikes on US bases in Middle East; Jordan bases take heaviest damage】Open-source intelligence group Faytuks Network reports Iran has stepped up missile strikes on US bases over the past week. Most missiles were intercepted and most Gulf bases sustained only minor damage, but strikes in Jordan have been more persistent and damaging. Despite interceptions, missiles have hit Jordanian targets nearly nightly since the escalation, a pattern that may indicate local air defenses are approaching saturation and interceptor shortages, which would reduce per-shot intercept effectiveness. Muwaffaq Salti Air Base — a key US power-projection node hosting multiple fighter squadrons, including F-15s and F-35s — has sustained multiple hits; satellite imagery shows two new impact points near a northern hangar and several destroyed structures. King Hussein (H5) Air Base has recorded at least two hits; imagery shows a destroyed hangar that may have housed aircraft (presence unknown) and craters and burn marks near the runway edge, implying damage to adjacent runway areas. Faytuks says most other attacks on US facilities have largely failed and so far had limited operational impact.

【古井貢古20領跌 白酒大單品總價連續五天重新整理近四個月新低】新浪財經“酒價內參”過去24小時收集的資料顯示，中國白酒市場主要大單品的終端零售均價7月18日整體繼續微幅回落，深陷低位連創新低。如果主要單品各取一瓶整體打包售賣，今日總售價為9805元，較昨日微跌1元，連續第七天回落，並連續第五日重新整理今年3月22日以來最低。縱觀全場，核心單品的漲跌分化依然顯著，終端交投量能基本穩定，總價低位震盪下行態勢延續。

今日11大白酒單品五漲五跌一平，漲跌家數均衡，但輸家跌幅略佔上風。上漲方面，青花郎漲幅居前，上漲5元/瓶，重返690元關口上方；飛天茅臺、五糧液普五八代、青花汾20與洋河夢之藍M6+均上漲3元/瓶，其中飛天茅臺連續兩日回升，但仍處於近四個月低位，隨著i茅臺平臺零售價上調至1639元/瓶，其相對官方定價的價差收窄至79元/瓶；五糧液普五八代終結此前連續四日的快速下探，自“酒價內參”去年11月有統計以來最低位處小幅修復，但仍處於780元關口下方；青花汾20連續兩日回升；洋河夢之藍M6+自近一月最低位連續兩日反彈，進一步向590元關口靠近。下跌方面，古井貢古20跌幅居前，下跌6元/瓶，連續兩日回落並失守530元關口，創7月份以來最低；國窖1573與習酒君品均下跌4元/瓶，其中後者價格再度跌破630元關口；精品茅臺與五糧液1618均下跌2元/瓶，其中後者重新失守820元關口。水晶劍南春價格環比昨日保持不變。

### 2026-07-18 07:23:23  #宏觀

【經濟日報：如何化解“車大位小”困局】近期，不少車主反映遭遇了“車大位小”的停車難題，想停進車位不容易，想開關車門也不方便。經濟日報刊文指出，化解“車大位小”困局，當務之急是統一標準、明確底線。目前，國內公共停車位的建設寬度尺寸主要是依據住建部2015年印發的《車庫建築設計規範》，該規範設計的小型車標準車位寬度僅為2.4米，而當前主流新能源SUV車身寬度普遍接近2米，兩車並排後剩餘空間極為有限。同時，該標準為推薦性標準，實際執行中常被打折扣。2024年，住建部已啟動《城市停車專項規劃標準》起草工作，新標準將更加強調車位對新能源汽車的適配性。在標準修訂過程中，應充分徵求各方意見，對新建專案設定硬性底線，對存量改造給予彈性空間。化解“車大位小”困局，還需系統統籌，推進長短期綜合施策。短期來看，可對具備條件的車位進行改造，滿足大車的停車需求。長期來看，推動城市規劃、住建、交通等部門建立協同機制，將車位配建標準納入土地出讓條件、專案驗收等硬約束。同時，在汽車生產和推介過程中，應進一步最佳化產品設計，強化輿論引導，破除“車大即高階”的片面認知，為消費者提供尺寸更合理、效能更均衡的汽車產品。

### 2026-07-18 05:02:00  #Trading Economics #Economy #Colombia #Consumer Confidence #Importance 1

Colombia Consumer Confidence Jumps in June — Colombia’s Consumer Confidence Index rose 6.5 percentage points to 24.3% in June 2026 from 17.8% in May. The Consumer Expectations Index climbed to 31.4% in June from 26.7% in May, while the Current Economic Conditions Index rose to 13.8% from 4.4%. Consumers became more optimistic about both their household finances and the country's economic outlook. The share of respondents expecting "good economic times" over the next 12 months increased to 31.3% from 21.1%, while expectations for an improvement in the national economy rose to 22.5% from 16.8%. Households also reported a better assessment of their current financial situation. The balance of respondents who said their household's financial condition was better than a year earlier increased to 11.5% from 3.1%. Meanwhile, willingness to purchase durable goods jumped to 16.1% from 5.8%.

Ibovespa Ends Slightly Lower as Financials Weigh — The Ibovespa inched 0.1% lower to close at 173,714 on Friday as losses in the financial sector weighed on the index, while gains in energy stocks partially offset the decline. The US and Iran intensified attacks across the Gulf, with their collapsed truce disrupting oil flows through the Strait of Hormuz. The conflict pushed oil prices higher, stoking inflation fears and weighing on financials and the broader market, while supporting energy stocks. Itaú lost 1.4%, Bradesco shed 0.6%, and both Itaúsa and Banco do Brasil retreated 1.3%, while Petrobras advanced 2.5%. Utility stocks traded mixed, with Axia up 0.9%, while Sabesp shed 0.3%. Steelmakers also traded mixed after the US announced a new 25% tariff on Brazilian imports. CSN lost 1.0%, while Gerdau added 0.5%. Meanwhile, the IBC-Br economic activity index rose 0.1% month-over-month in May 2026, easing from a 0.5% increase in the previous month but slightly exceeding forecasts for a flat reading.

Mexican Peso Retreats as Risk Aversion Boosts Dollar — The Mexican peso weakened to 17.52 per USD in July after reaching 17.40 earlier in the month, as escalating tensions in the Middle East fueled risk aversion across global financial markets. The escalation prompted investors to seek safe-haven assets such as the US dollar. Meanwhile, Mexico's industrial output contracted by more than expected in May, with weakness broad-based across sectors, adding pressure to the peso amid signs of a slowing economy. Oil prices also extended gains as tensions near the Strait of Hormuz intensified, fueling energy-driven inflation fears. In contrast, Mexico's annual inflation rate eased to 3.37% in June 2026 from 3.94% in May, the lowest reading since December 2020. The figure came in slightly below market expectations of 3.52% and remained within the Bank of Mexico's target tolerance range of one percentage point around 3%.

US Stocks Fall as Chip Selloff Deepens — US stock indices closed lower on Friday amid a fresh selloff in chipmakers and renewed geopolitical tensions. The S&P 500 lost 1%, the Nasdaq shed 1.5%, and the Dow Jones retreated 407 points. Semiconductor manufacturers plunged on concerns that AI hyperscalers may scale back investment in AI infrastructure, reversing part of this year's rally. Fears of weaker capital expenditure were also fueled by improvements in Chinese AI models, including Moonshot's latest Kimi release. Nvidia lost 2.2%, Broadcom shed 1%, AMD fell 1%, and Intel retreated 2%. Meanwhile, inflationary risks gained momentum as the war in the Middle East continued and fuel prices rose. Economic headwinds were also reinforced after US President Trump claimed China had interfered in the 2020 US presidential election, raising concerns over the durability of the trade truce reached following last year's tariff exchanges. On the earnings front, Netflix sank 7.3% after forecasting another quarter of slowing sales.

### 2026-07-18 01:38:20  #國際

【美銀：關鍵指標極度看漲，建議拋售股票】美國銀行由邁克爾·哈特內特（Michael Hartnett）領導的策略師團隊表示，資產配置者已變得極度看漲，這通常是市場的一個警告訊號，表明投資者應該“減少股票敞口、撤退或進行板塊輪動”，而不是繼續加倉風險資產。 哈特內特在週五的一份報告中寫道，美國銀行的內部情緒指標——“牛熊指標”（Bull & Bear Indicator）正在閃爍逆向的“賣出訊號”。這主要歸因於“機構現金水平驟降、強勁的股票資金流入”以及全球股指廣度的增強。該指標已攀升至9.6，進一步推入極度看漲區域。而在大約兩個月前，該指標讀數為8.0，這通常意味著市場因擁擠的看漲倉位而過熱。美銀表示，該指標低於2.0的閾值意味著買入訊號。

US Dollar Index Holds Bounce — The US dollar index rose to 100.8, rebounding from the one-month low of 100.5 on July 16th and diverging with the retreat for Treasury yields amid a pivot to safer assets. Trade jitters with China retook the spotlight after President Trump claimed that China interfered with the US 2020 presidential elections. The claims jeopardized their truce in economic relations since the exchange of tariffs softened last year, magnifying ongoing shipping bottlenecks amid the war in the Middle East. Trade disputes were also featured between the EU and China, dampening demand for the Euro. On the monetary policy front, FOMC members continued to signal that evidence of stubborn core inflation may warrant a rate hike this year, backed by recent evidence of strong retail sales and low unemployment claim levels. The hawkish remarks prevailed despite soft inflation data for June and a second decline in inflation expectations per the Michigan consumer confidence survey.

【香港特區政府歡迎立法會透過《皇崗口岸港方口岸區條例草案》 】香港特區政府17日表示，歡迎特區立法會當日透過《皇崗口岸港方口岸區條例草案》。草案的順利透過，是落實中央相關授權的重要里程碑，為在重建後的皇崗口岸實施“一地兩檢”安排提供法律基礎，更標誌著口岸開通的籌備工作邁出關鍵一步。 國務院於7月8日就啟用皇崗口岸港方口岸區及相關延伸區有關事項作出批覆，確定港方口岸區的具體座標和麵積，並同意港方口岸區由2026年7月31日0時起啟用並依照香港特別行政區法律實施管轄。為確保中央的授權得以全面落實，特區政府有責任於7月31日前完成相關的本地立法工作。 據悉，《皇崗口岸港方口岸區條例》將於7月20日刊憲，並於7月31日生效，為特區在皇崗口岸設立港方口岸區，以及在港方口岸區依法實施管轄提供法律基礎。（新華社）

### 2026-07-17 22:33:58  #MKT News #Macro & Rates #The Fed (FOMC) #S&P500 #Impact bullish #DXY #Impact mixed

【Dollar hedging costs fall to lowest level this year】Cost of hedging dollar volatility has dropped to its lowest level this year. This week the one‑month implied volatility on the dollar spot index fell to its lowest since December, reversing much of the spike after the March outbreak of the Iran war. Despite Fed policy uncertainty and renewed Middle East tensions, traders signal a very low probability of a major shock to the global reserve currency. The calm is reinforcing a key market theme: US equity resilience and reduced currency volatility are drawing flows into arbitrage and carry trades that profit from interest‑rate differentials when FX and risk appetite are stable. ING FX strategist Francesco Pesole called the fall in dollar volatility striking and said AI‑driven equity resilience appears to be stabilising exchange rates and sustaining a self‑reinforcing low‑volatility, carry‑dominated environment; he added that a tech sell‑off would likely not deter demand for these strategies.

### 2026-07-17 22:32:49  #MKT News

【Lithium batteries to face consumption tax like refined fuel】Broker analysts say a consumption tax on lithium batteries was widely anticipated and industry expects limited impact. Battery cell prices are about 0.35–0.40 yuan/Wh; a 2% consumption tax would add roughly 0.007–0.008 yuan/Wh. A 2–4% tax range corresponds to an equivalent 12,000–25,000 yuan/ton effect on lithium carbonate. Lithium carbonate prices have been rising and orders remain supply-constrained, so the tax’s incremental burden is small. For end buyers, a 2–4% levy would raise per‑vehicle costs by only a few hundred yuan, well below prior lithium-driven cost swings. CPCA secretary-general Cui Dongshu said the move signals that “parity between oil and electricity” is being established; together with earlier purchase‑tax cuts and the removal of vehicle-and-vessel tax exemptions, the sector’s tax preferences are being phased out.

【China's Ministry of Agriculture launches 100-day campaign to protect autumn grain during peak flood season】Entering the main flood period of late July–early August, when agricultural disasters and autumn-grain yield formation are most acute, the Ministry of Agriculture has launched a 100-day field-management and disaster-resilience campaign to secure the autumn and annual grain harvests. The drive targets key crops, vulnerable regions and major disaster events with crop-, region- and stage-specific measures: scientific fertilization, water-saving irrigation, foliar sprays and other yield-enhancement field practices. It directs ditch clearing and soil-moisture management, chemical measures to control lodging and single foliar-spray operations intended to deliver multiple protective effects. Authorities will implement unified and joint prevention-and-control and emergency-response arrangements to minimise disaster losses and protect harvests.

【July U.S. consumer sentiment rises; Middle East flare-up raises downside risk】University of Michigan consumer sentiment rose to 54.4 in July, the highest since February, versus a June final of 49.5 and economists’ forecast of 51.0. The survey ran June 23–July 13; more than 70% of interviews were completed before last week’s breakdown of a U.S.-Iran ceasefire that pushed oil to a one-month high and lifted gasoline prices. The survey director Joanne Xu said the July gain was broad-based across age, income, wealth and political affiliation, but consumers remain downbeat on the outlook—sentiment is 12% lower than a year ago—and the confidence upswing may not persist if gasoline prices resume rising.

US 10-Year Yield Declines — The yield on the 10-year US Treasury note fell to 4.52% from the near two-month high of 5.62% on July 13th, amid softer inflation data and a decline in risk sentiment. Both consumer and producer inflation decline in June, while inflation expectations measured by the Michigan survey dropped for a second month, suggesting that the pullback in wholesale fuel costs are being transmitted to the economy. Fixed-income assets were also supported by new geopolitical uncertainty after US President Trump claimed China compromised US presidential elections in 2020, risking the truce between both countries since the exchange of tariff hikes last year. Still, pro-inflationary risks lingered as commercial vessels refrained from crossing the Strait of Hormuz due to new strikes between Iran and the US. Rate futures suggested that over two thirds of the market are positioned for a Fed hike by the end of the year, although this month's decision is expected to be a hold.

US 1-Year Inflation Outlook Hits 4-Month Low — The year-ahead inflation expectations in the United States, as compiled by the survey of the University of Michigan, fell for the second month to 4.2% in July 2026, the lowest since March, down from 4.6% in June, preliminary estimates showed. In the meantime, the five-year inflation outlook stood at a three-month low of 3.3% in July, unchanged from June.

### 2026-07-17 22:05:51  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Consumer Confidence #Importance 3

US Consumer Sentiment Improves for Second Straight Month — The University of Michigan's Consumer Sentiment Index rose to 54.4 in July 2026, beating expectations of 51.0 and marking a second straight monthly increase after May's record low, according to the preliminary estimate. Sentiment reached its highest level since February, supported by easing gasoline prices. All five index components improved, led by roughly 20% gains in buying conditions for durable goods and year-ahead business conditions. The improvement was broad-based across age, income, wealth, and political groups, with particularly strong gains among consumers without a bachelor's degree. Despite the rebound, sentiment remains 12% below its level a year ago as elevated prices continue to weigh on households. Meanwhile, one-year inflation expectations eased to 4.2% from 4.6%, while long-run expectations held steady at 3.3%. Most survey responses were collected before the July 7 resumption of US strikes against Iran and the subsequent rise in gasoline prices.

### 2026-07-17 22:01:50  #MKT News #Commodities #Energy #WTI #Impact bullish #Brent #XAUUSD

【US strikes on Iranian bridges, water plants and granaries broaden targets; analysts flag higher Strait of Hormuz oil and shipping risk】US strikes against Iran have increasingly hit civilian infrastructure — bridges, water-treatment facilities and grain stores — prompting analysts to warn of heightened oil and shipping risk and a broader shift in US targeting. King's College senior lecturer Mark Hilborne says many struck sites are dual-use but clearly affect civilians; he highlights bridges as key to southern military logistics and to Iran's ability to move equipment and threaten shipping. Hilborne adds a northeast bridge struck earlier sits on a strategic trade corridor used for economic traffic and alleged weapons-part shipments. Attacks have concentrated on Bandar Abbas, home to Iran's conventional navy and the IRGC naval base overlooking the Strait of Hormuz; more than 90% of Iran's oil exports transit the strait, making Bandar Abbas both a military and economic choke point. London military analyst Alex Alfirraz Scheers says expanding strikes beyond conventional military targets may signal a wider strategic shift — aimed at enforcing a maritime blockade, degrading military logistics and pressuring Iran to negotiate — and could represent early steps toward preparing limited ground operations. Lancaster University professor Simon Mabon cautions the pattern could be interpreted as a precursor to ground intervention but calls a full-scale invasion unlikely and warns attacks on civilian infrastructure may strengthen Tehran's domestic narrative and raise the risk of further escalation. Market takeaway: strikes widening to infrastructure in southern Iran and at Bandar Abbas raise the risk premium on crude flows and regional shipping security, warranting close monitoring for disruption to exports and any signs of further escalation.

【Rate‑hike bets and fund rotation pull tech leaders lower; semis enter bear market】US stocks opened lower Friday as broad tech weakness hit markets. The Philadelphia Semiconductor Index is more than 20% below its record closing high and looks set to confirm a bear market. SK Hynix ADR fell below its $149 issue price for the first time. SpaceX shares are down about 38% from their peak, erasing roughly $1 trillion of peak value. AMD dropped over 7%, Intel over 6%, and TSMC over 5%. Analysts say the chip rout has exceeded fundamentals and is driven more by fund/style rotation than company-level news. Hawkish signals from Fed officials pushed up rate-hike expectations: Fed chair Kevin Warsh’s critical remarks on inflation at a hearing tightened markets, while other Fed officials signalled hawkish intent — Fed official Logan explicitly called for hikes and Fed official Schmid warned the risk of inflation accelerating in coming months remains. Continued uncertainty over US‑Iran war prospects has also dented risk appetite.

TSX Slips on Tech Rout and Middle East Tensions — The S&P/TSX Composite Index fell nearly 1% to trade below 35,500 on Friday amid a global selloff in semiconductor stocks and as the escalating US-Iran conflict kept oil prices elevated. Shopify shed 1.5%, while Celestica extended losses of more than 6% after plunging 9.3% in the previous session. Meanwhile, the US and Iran intensified attacks across the Gulf, with their collapsed truce disrupting oil flows through the Strait of Hormuz. The conflict pushed oil prices higher, stoking inflation fears and weighing on financials and the broader market. RBC, TD Bank, BMO, and Scotiabank lost about 1% each. Gold prices also edged lower, with the precious metal heading for a weekly loss, putting additional pressure on mining stocks. Agnico Eagle lost more than 1%, while Barrick and WPM shed nearly 2% each.

Baltic Dry Index at 2-Week Low — The Baltic Exchange's dry bulk freight index, which monitors rates for ships carrying dry bulk commodities, retreated for the third session on Friday, slipping about 3.1% to its lowest since July 3 at 2,752 points. The capesize index, which typically transports 150,000-ton cargoes including iron ore and coal, also declined for the third day, dropping 5.6% to 4,097 points, as weaker cargo enquiries and increased vessel availability pressured spot earnings. At the same time, the panamax index, which tracks vessels carrying around 60,000 to 70,000 tons of coal or grain, fell by 0.4% to 2,248 points. Among smaller vessels, the supramax index rose 0.4% to a fresh high since August 2022 at 1,737 points. For the week, the benchmark index was down more than 6%.

【Apple overtakes NVIDIA as world's most valuable company】On Friday Apple (AAPL.O) overtook NVIDIA (NVDA.O) as the world's most valuable company. Apple's intraday market value stood at $4.88 trillion; NVIDIA shares fell 3.5%, putting its market value at about $4.86 trillion. The ranking change reflects investors re-evaluating which firms benefit from the AI boom beyond the most obvious winners and is the first time Apple has held the top spot since April last year. Tony Meadows, chief investment officer at BRI Wealth Management, said Apple had been viewed as an AI laggard for not investing in model development but market sentiment has shifted; he said Apple's lower capex dependence and its ability to monetise AI through services, ecosystem lock‑in and hardware upgrades supports a valuation reset that reflects confidence in earnings sustainability rather than speculative AI upside.

【此猴非彼猴！這種炒作離大譜】7月17日，“猴”概念股集體降溫。截至當日收盤，益諾思跌超14%，昭衍新藥A股股價跌停，美迪西、康龍化成、藥明康德等熱門“猴”概念股紛紛下挫。 值得注意的是，“猴”概念爆火後，有不少網友將實驗猴炒作成了“猴模組”，把峨眉山稱為“猴儲存”，甚至連帶著給果蔬企業宏輝果蔬都編出了“光刻蕉”的諧音梗。7月15日，宏輝果蔬收穫漲停，有網友給出的解釋是“宏輝果蔬賣香蕉，猴子愛吃香蕉”；以文旅主業聞名的峨眉山A則因景區裡猴子多，被網友強行劃入“猴”概念陣營，近期連續收漲。 這種以諧音、聯想為依據的炒作路徑，或存在市場情緒過熱和非理性炒作的風險。今日，隨著“猴”概念股集體降溫，峨眉山A、宏輝果蔬也隨之一同下挫。（上海證券報微信公眾號）

【U.S. June factory output stalls as durable-goods slump drags】U.S. manufacturing output stalled in June, weighed down by a drop in durable-goods production. Factory output, which accounts for roughly three-quarters of total industrial production, was flat month-on-month in June after May was revised to +0.1%; economists had expected +0.1%. Overall industrial production rose 0.1% for the second consecutive month; utilities and mining each climbed 0.4%. Within durables, output of wood products, machinery, electrical equipment and household appliances fell, while growth in computers & electronics and defense & aerospace slowed. Other recent data show some manufacturing pickup, but renewed U.S.-Iran conflict poses upside risk to energy prices that could raise input costs and suppress demand.

Silver Hits Multi-Month Low on Fed Rate Outlook — Silver fell to around $55 an ounce on Friday, its lowest level since late November 2025, as rising oil prices fueled inflation concerns and reinforced expectations that the Federal Reserve will keep interest rates higher for longer. Geopolitical tensions escalated after Iran launched fresh strikes on US facilities in the Middle East, following a sixth consecutive night of US attacks on Iranian military targets that continued to disrupt shipping through the Strait of Hormuz. Adding to the pressure, Dallas Fed President Lorie Logan called for a rate hike, while Fed Vice Chair Philip Jefferson said he would support tighter policy if inflation failed to show near-term improvement. Meanwhile, data released during the week showed US consumer and producer prices both declined in June, largely reflecting lower energy costs, while import prices unexpectedly increased.

### 2026-07-17 21:30:37  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Capacity Utilization #Importance 1

US Capacity Utilization Stable for Third Month — US capacity utilization stood at 76.1% in June 2026, unchanged from the previous month following a downward revision and slightly below market expectations of 76.2%. The reading marked a third consecutive month of stable capacity utilization, remaining 3.3 percentage points below its long-run average for 1972–2025. Among the major sectors, the operating rate for utilities edged up 0.2 percentage points to 69.5%, though it remained 14.5 percentage points below its historical average. Mining utilization also increased, rising 0.4 percentage points to 87.4%. By contrast, manufacturing capacity utilization edged down to 75.7%, remaining 2.5 percentage points below its long-run average.

【市場監管總局出手嚴管拼豆！面向14歲以下兒童使用的“拼豆”產品：拼豆包裝須標註適用年齡】央視財經記者今天（17日）瞭解到，為保障消費者人身健康安全，依照《中華人民共和國產品質量法》《中華人民共和國未成年人保護法》等法律法規，市場監管總局釋出“拼豆”產品質量安全有關事項公告。其中，面向14歲以下兒童使用的“拼豆”產品：應當透過玩具強制性產品認證（CCC認證），不得以“搭售”“贈品”等方式，在玩具產品出廠時違規搭配家用電器；產品應當在顯著位置標註產品適用年齡、安全警示等注意事項，明示家長購買注意事項，提醒家長履行兒童監護責任，按照適用年齡選擇合適玩具，嚴禁模糊標註、虛假標註。 市場監管總局要求：“拼豆”產品生產者應當嚴格按照相關法律法規要求組織生產，加強質量管控。銷售者應當嚴格落實產品質量安全主體責任，履行進貨查驗義務。電商平臺應當加強對平臺內銷售“拼豆”產品的核驗核查，對發現的違規產品立即採取必要處置措施，並依法向市場監管部門報告。

### 2026-07-17 21:06:37  #宏觀 #國際

【外交部：將推動世界人工智慧合作組織儘早啟動運轉】7月16日，成立世界人工智慧合作組織協定簽署儀式在上海舉行，29個國家代表簽署協定，成為創始成員國。外交部發言人林劍17日下午在例行記者會上介紹有關情況時表示，倡議成立世界人工智慧合作組織是中方推動構建人類命運共同體，落實全球治理倡議的又一生動實踐。下階段，中方將會同各創始成員國推動組織儘早啟動運轉。 記者會上，有記者提問：昨天，成立世界人工智慧合作組織協定簽署儀式在上海舉行。中方如何看待成立該組織的意義和影響？該組織同聯合國框架下的人工智慧治理平臺是什麼關係？ 林劍介紹，7月16日，成立世界人工智慧合作組織協定的簽署儀式在上海舉行。中共中央政治局委員、外交部長王毅出席並代表中國政府簽署協定。哈薩克、寮國、巴基斯坦、俄羅斯、印度尼西亞等29個國家代表簽署協定，成為創始成員國。聯合國秘書長古特雷斯等國家和國際組織代表出席儀式。 林劍表示，中方始終積極踐行習近平主席提出的《全球人工智慧治理倡議》，秉持以人為本、向上向善理念，推動人工智慧全球治理。正如習近平主席今天上午在2026世界人工智慧大會暨人工智慧全球治理高階別會議開幕式上指出的，世界人工智慧合作組織在上海應運而生，這是中方響應全球南方呼聲，團結國際社會，積極推動人工智慧發展和治理的重大舉措，將成為人工智慧發展史上的一個重要里程碑。 林劍說，中方一貫主張在聯合國框架下推進人工智慧全球治理，推動聯合國大會協商一致透過人工智慧能力建設國際合作決議，支援落實全球數字契約和聯合國可持續發展目標。 林劍表示，中方願同各方一道，遵循聯合國憲章宗旨，秉持共商、共建、共享理念，堅持以人為本原則，加強人工智慧發展戰略、治理規則、技術標準的對接協調，促進人工智慧國際合作和全球治理，確保人工智慧朝著有益、安全、公平方向健康有序發展。（新華社）

【US housing starts rebound on multifamily surge】US housing starts rose 19% in June to a 1.43 mln annualized rate, the strongest since March and above economist forecasts, government data showed. Multifamily starts surged about 76% to a 532k annualized rate after a near-40% drop the prior month; single-family starts slipped 0.2%. High home prices and elevated mortgage rates are suppressing single-family demand but appear to be supporting apartment building. Builders report high single-family inventory and weak demand, prompting sales incentives. Forward-looking permits fell 3% to the lowest since March; single-family permits hit a 10-month low and multifamily permits also declined, signalling downside risk to future construction activity.

【美國6月份新屋開工數反彈 多戶型住宅建設激增】美國6月份新屋開工數在前一個月大幅下降後激增，主要受公寓建設反彈推動。週五釋出的官方資料顯示，6月份新屋開工數增加19%，摺合年率為143萬套，創3月份以來最高水平。該資料高於調查中所有經濟學家的預期。多戶住宅開工數增長超過76%，摺合年率為53.2萬套，此前一個月該資料暴跌近40%。與此同時，單戶住宅開工數下降0.2%，在建築商經歷整體低迷的春季之後再次下滑。多戶住宅建設反彈凸顯出月度資料的波動性，尤其是在公寓領域。不過，高房價和高抵押貸款利率一直壓制獨棟住宅需求，而這些因素也可能在支撐公寓需求。與此同時，獨棟住宅建築商總體上一直面臨庫存高企和需求疲軟的局面。這迫使許多建築商透過銷售激勵措施來吸引買家。

### 2026-07-17 20:53:29  #MKT News #Commodities #Energy #Precious Metals #S&P500 #Impact bearish #WTI #Impact bullish #XAUUSD

【Wealth manager warns of 'generation-level' bear; says US stocks could fall at least 40%】Oxbow Advisors founder Ted Oakley warned US equities could decline at least 40% — and possibly more — in a 'generation-level' bear that could last one to two years. He said valuations sit about three standard deviations above normal and that mean reversion would require a 40–45% drop; chasing a final 6–8% upside now carries roughly 25% downside risk. Oakley flagged semiconductor concentration risk (10–12 names account for roughly half the market) and used Intel's multi-decade recovery after 1999 as a caution against late-cycle buying. Oxbow's equity exposure is slightly above 60% across 45 stocks, with the remainder in short-term Treasuries. The firm is overweight energy and commodities — Oakley said he would be surprised if oil does not return above $100, arguing production-side disruption is worse than widely recognized — and expects gold, miners and silver to rebound next year as momentum unwinds. CIO Chance Finucane said the firm is cautious on 2027 because next year will face a high 1H base, which could slow growth and produce disinflation that is not supportive for risk assets.

【工信部組織召開重點汽車生產企業座談會 要求汽車生產企業堅決抵制非理性競爭、加強產品測試驗證與安全評估】7月17日，工業和資訊化部裝備工業一司組織召開重點汽車生產企業座談會，部署進一步規範汽車產業競爭秩序、提升汽車產品生產一致性和質量安全水平、開展汽車產品安全風險隱患排查和監督檢查等重點工作。會議要求，汽車生產企業要深入貫徹落實黨中央、國務院決策部署，堅決抵制非理性競爭，加強產品測試驗證與安全評估，守牢產品生產一致性和質量安全底線。一是要深入開展產品安全風險隱患排查，對照有關管理要求，系統查詢企業自身和關鍵零部件供應商在產品生產一致性、可靠性、耐久性和新技術測試驗證等方面存在的問題，認真做好整改工作，全面加強供應鏈管理，強化生產一致性保證能力。二是要加強產品創新設計測試驗證，充分評估有關安全風險，穩妥推動新技術裝車應用，確保符合有關國家標準要求，以及保障人體健康和人身、財產安全的要求，嚴格履行告知義務，不得誇大和虛假宣傳。三是要加強組合駕駛輔助和自動駕駛功能安全評估，進一步強化企業在功能安全、預期功能安全、網路安全、資料安全、軟體升級等方面的安全保障能力，加強安全事件監測和處置。會議強調，汽車生產企業要牢固樹立“安全第一、質量至上”的理念，切實承擔起保障產品質量安全和維護公共安全、公民生命財產安全的責任，圍繞產品設計驗證、生產製造、售後服務等方面做好風險防範，切實維護消費者合法權益。下一步，工業和資訊化部將會同相關部門深入開展道路機動車輛產品生產一致性和質量提升行動，集中整治突出問題，進一步加強汽車產品創新設計准入審查和測試驗證有關工作，開展道路機動車輛生產企業產品安全保障能力和生產一致性監督檢查，加強道路機動車輛檢驗檢測機構管理，對於存在問題的道路機動車輛生產企業、檢驗檢測機構，依法依規嚴肅處理，堅決守牢汽車產品安全底線。

### 2026-07-17 20:48:55  #Trading Economics #Economy #Canada #Foreign Stock Investment #Importance 1

Foreign Investment in Canadian Securities Eases — Foreign investors acquired C$7.9 billion in Canadian securities in May 2026, down sharply from C$46.9 billion in April and well below market expectations of C$15.21 billion. Non-resident investors sold C$16.1 billion of Canadian equity securities, marking the largest monthly divestment since February 2025. The selloff was concentrated in the energy and mining (C$7.7 billion) and manufacturing (C$4.9 billion) sectors. Despite the equity outflows, foreign investors remained net buyers of Canadian securities for a fifth consecutive month, supported by acquisitions of federal government bonds (C$7.8 billion) and provincial government bonds (C$7.1 billion). Foreign investors also increased their holdings of money market instruments by C$6.0 billion, the largest investment since September 2025, driven primarily by purchases of private corporate paper (C$3.7 billion) and federal government business enterprise paper (C$2.8 billion).

Goldman forecasts average monthly purchases of 50t this year and 40t next year.

### 2026-07-17 20:38:20  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Import Prices MoM #Importance 1

US Import Prices Unexpectedly Rise — Prices of goods imported into the US rose by 0.3% from the previous month in June of 2026, slowing from the revised 1.7% in May but contrasting sharply with market expectations of a 0.7% decline. Imports of fuel and lubricants fell by 0.4% as the momentary pickup in tanker volumes leaving the Persian Gulf allowed for some relief in wholesale fuel oil and refined fuel costs. The slight drop compared to a 12.6% surge in May and an 18.9% jump in April. In turn, non-fuel import prices rose by 0.4%, extending the 0.7% increase in the previous month to mark a seventh consecutive period of higher prices. The increase in import costs do not include tariffs that have recently been raised by the presidential administration. From the previous month, import prices rose by 7.1%, the highest in nearly four years.

### 2026-07-17 20:34:44  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Export Prices MoM #Importance 1

US Export Prices Post First Monthly Decline in Over a Year — US export prices fell 0.6% month-over-month in June 2026, reversing a downwardly revised 1.2% increase in May and exceeding expectations for a 0.4% decline. It marked the first monthly drop since May 2025, driven by a 0.7% fall in nonagricultural export prices, as lower prices for industrial supplies and materials outweighed gains in capital goods, consumer goods, and automotive products. Meanwhile, agricultural export prices rose 0.2%, extending gains for a sixth straight month, supported by higher prices for other foods and food preparations and nuts, which more than offset declines in soybean and corn prices. On an annual basis, US export prices increased 10.2% in June.

Brazil's IBC-Br Growth Slows in May — Brazil’s IBC-Br economic activity index rose 0.1% month-over-month in May 2026, easing from a 0.5% increase in the previous month but slightly above forecasts for a flat reading. Excluding agriculture and livestock, the IBC-Br rose 0.2% in May.

### 2026-07-17 20:15:07  #焦點 #國際

【商務部新聞發言人就美不再延續涉港國家緊急狀態答記者問】有記者問：關注到截至目前，美國未再延續7月14日到期的《香港正常化總統行政令》（第13936號）中的涉港國家緊急狀態，這是否意味著涉港行政令已經終止？中方對此有何評論？

答：中方注意到有關情況。在中美馬德里經貿磋商中，美方就有關涉港、投資等問題作出承諾。近期，美方已向中方確認，第13936號行政令中的涉港國家緊急狀態今年到期後不再延續，該行政令將終止。美方有關行動是履行雙方經貿磋商共識的重要一步，中方對此表示讚賞。

維護香港繁榮穩定符合中美共同利益。美方涉港政策向積極方向調整，也符合國際社會的普遍期待。希望美方恪守有關國際公約和雙方共識，尊重中國主權和香港特區法治，恢復並加強與香港特區的正常經貿往來，為構建中美建設性戰略穩定關係發揮積極作用。

【何立峰在內蒙古調研時指出高質量建設自貿試驗區加快構建向北開放重要橋頭堡】中共中央政治局委員、國務院副總理何立峰7月14日至16日在內蒙古調研外經貿工作。何立峰指出，要深入學習貫徹習近平總書記對內蒙古工作的重要指示精神，認真落實黨中央、國務院決策部署，高質量建設內蒙古自貿試驗區，增強口岸經濟發展動能，不斷深化國際經貿合作，加快構建向北開放重要橋頭堡。 何立峰先後到中國（內蒙古）自由貿易試驗區滿洲里片區和海拉爾等地，實地調研自貿試驗區和經濟開發區建設、鐵路公路口岸通關、中歐班列運營、陸港國際物流中心發展等工作，深入瞭解相關外經貿和製造業企業生產經營、外貿進出口等情況。 何立峰指出，設立內蒙古自貿試驗區是完善國家對外開放格局、築牢向北開放平臺載體的重要舉措。要抓住自貿試驗區建設有利契機，加強體制機制創新，深化多雙邊經貿合作，推動共建“一帶一路”和中蒙俄經濟走廊建設走深走實。要加快構建高水平互聯互通網路，持續完善鐵路公路多式聯運服務，在確保安全的前提下，不斷提高口岸通關綜合服務效能。要充分發揮中歐班列、陸港國際物流中心等作用，積極推動跨境電商和跨境物流發展，進一步促進通道經濟向樞紐經濟和產業經濟升級。(新華社)

【伊朗襲擊科威特水處理廠和發電廠 以報復美國軍事打擊】作為對美國軍事打擊的反擊行動，伊朗襲擊了科威特的水廠和發電廠，這凸顯出中東衝突正在惡化，民用設施受到越來越大的威脅。科威特政府週五表示，一座海水淡化和發電廠遭到襲擊，多臺發電機組受損。儘管科威特的此類設施在3月至4月初美伊戰爭最激烈時曾遭襲，但在最新這一輪衝突升級期間，這是德黑蘭首次將電力基礎設施作為打擊目標。科威特電力和水務部在X上表示，有關部門正在努力讓受損機組恢復執行，也已經撲滅了襲擊引發的一起火災。該部敦促居民節約用電，以緩解電網壓力。作為全球最炎熱國家之一，科威特在夏季期間電網通常處於高負荷狀態。本週末該國就可能迎來50攝氏度的高溫。

【India proposes tighter passenger-car fuel-efficiency rules to cut oil imports】India's Ministry of Power has proposed draft fuel-efficiency standards for passenger cars covering vehicles produced over the next five years through March 2032, with fuel-consumption limits set to tighten annually. The measures, aimed at reducing crude imports and cutting vehicle emissions—a major source of urban pollution—will be finalised after public consultation. Contracts or the S&P 500 fell nearly 1% and those for the Nasdaq 100 lost 2%, while the Dow was 350 points down. Micron and Intel dropped more than 3% premarket, while Nvidia lost 2%. Meanwhile, pro-inflationary risks gathered momentum as the war in the Middle East continued and fuel costs picked up. Economic headwinds were also stemmed by US President Trump claiming China interfered in the US 2020 presidential elections, risking the truce between both countries since last year's tariff's exchanges. ### 2026-07-17 19:49:12  #國際

【習近平會見聯合國秘書長古特雷斯】7月17日下午，國家主席習近平在上海西郊賓館會見來華出席2026世界人工智慧大會暨人工智慧全球治理高階別會議的聯合國秘書長古特雷斯。

習近平指出，古特雷斯先生擔任聯合國秘書長10年來，領導聯合國堅定捍衛多邊主義、積極應對氣候變化等全球性挑戰、推動人工智慧等新興領域全球治理、開啟聯合國改革程序，中方予以高度評價。今年是新中國恢復聯合國合法席位55週年。55年來，中國堅持做世界和平的建設者、全球發展的貢獻者、國際秩序的維護者，始終堅定支援聯合國。我提出構建人類命運共同體理念和四大全球倡議，一個重要考慮就是維護聯合國地位和權威。中方將一如既往同聯合國深化合作，促進世界和平與發展事業，推動構建人類命運共同體。

習近平強調，當前，國際形勢變和亂的一面更加突出，需要進一步踐行真正的多邊主義、重振聯合國地位和作用。一是堅持公道正義。每個國家都是國際社會平等一員，每個國家的主權、領土完整和合法權益都應該得到尊重，決不能讓世界重回叢林法則時代。二是堅持守正創新。守正就是要遵守聯合國憲章宗旨，維護好第二次世界大戰結束後確立的國際體系和國際規則；創新就是要推動聯合國因時而變，勇於自我革新，進一步提質增效。三是堅持擔當作為。全球190多個國家乘坐在一條命運與共的大船上，應該同舟共濟、攜手應對全球性挑戰。大國應該承擔更多責任，展現更大格局。中國始終以實際行動積極推動探索新時代大國正確相處之道，為世界注入更多穩定性和確定性。

古特雷斯表示，在習近平主席堅強領導下，無論國際形勢多麼困難複雜，中國始終堅定支援多邊主義、支援聯合國事業、推動國際合作，為世界樹立了榜樣。中國一直致力於糾正發展不平衡、應對氣候變化、推動智慧向善、彌合發展鴻溝、維護公道正義，提出一系列重大理念和倡議，令人欽佩。聯合國將繼續加強同中方的合作，反對單邊主義、保護主義和霸道霸凌，維護聯合國憲章和國際法，推動世界多極化程序，維護國際社會共同利益。

蔡奇、丁薛祥、王毅、陳吉寧等參加會見。（新華社）

【Six ministries issue '15th Five-Year' plan for soil, groundwater and agricultural-rural ecological protection】Six ministries led by the Ministry of Ecology and Environment issued a '15th Five-Year' plan for soil, groundwater and agricultural-rural ecological environment protection. The plan sets five priority tasks: tighten risk control over construction land, strengthen ecological protection of farmland, deepen coordinated prevention and control of groundwater pollution, advance rural (beautiful countryside) construction, and enhance governance effectiveness. It allocates major projects including a soil pollution source-prevention engineering program and a special soil remediation program for chemical-site plots vacated within 1 km of rivers, and mandates a second national soil-pollution survey and a nationwide groundwater-pollution investigation and assessment.

【Zimbabwe rejects producers' request to delay lithium concentrate export ban】Zimbabwe Mines Minister Kambamura said the government has rejected the lithium industry's request to postpone a lithium concentrate export ban set to take effect on January 1 until at least March, and that the January 1 timetable remains. Harsh measures were first introduced in February to force more domestic value-added processing and curb illegal exports of metallic lithium for EV batteries; export restrictions were partly eased in April, but the ban is planned for formal implementation in early 2027. The Zimbabwe Lithium Producers Association had sought a multi-month grace period; Kambamura said producers were notified as early as June 2025 and must comply with the policy.

【中國光伏行業協會徵集行業專家開展太空光伏領域專項研究】隨著航天技術與商業航天的快速發展，太空光伏作為航天器能源核心，正成為支撐空間探測、衛星通訊及空間太陽能電站等戰略任務的關鍵技術。同時，低空經濟、高空無人機等新興場景的拓展，也對極端環境下光伏系統的高效化、輕量化和高可靠性提出更高要求，推動太空光伏向空天融合應用延伸。作為光伏與航天深度融合的前沿領域，太空光伏是我國搶佔能源戰略高點、拓展光伏應用新空間的重要方向，開發更低成本太空光伏技術，以支援太空光伏市場未來規模化發展，需要匯聚光伏、航空航天、衛星等各方資源協同推進。為此，中國光伏行業協會擬面向全社會公開徵集專家，共同開展太空光伏產業的政策研究、技術攻關、標準編制等工作。

【本週主力加碼PCB個股】據證券時報·資料寶統計，本週（7月13日至17日），A股市場共計32股主力資金淨流入額超過3億元。滬電股份、鵬鼎控股、比亞迪、星網銳捷、寧德時代排名前五，淨流入額依次為14.85億元、13.73億元、12.56億元、11.92億元、10.02億元。滬電股份預計上半年實現歸母淨利潤28.3億元至30億元，同比增長68.17%至78.28%。公司表示，受益於高速交換機、AI伺服器、高效能運算及智慧汽車等應用領域的結構性需求，疊加產品結構的持續最佳化，上半年公司營業收入和淨利潤較上年同期均有較快增長。值得一提的是，主力資金淨流入前兩名的滬電股份和鵬鼎控股均為PCB板塊個股，同屬於PCB概念的景旺電子主力資金淨流入額也高達6.82億元。訊息面上，當前，全球PCB行業整體保持穩健增長。根據Prismark統計，2024年全球PCB市場規模約為750億美元，預計2029年將增長至930億美元以上，年複合增速約4%—5%。

【德銀：人民幣國際化邁入“雙引擎”驅動的全新發展階段】德意志銀行研究最新報告稱，在明確的政策支援和市場結構的持續最佳化下，人民幣國際化正進入一個由“雙引擎”驅動的全新發展階段。歐洲機構成為人民幣融資市場國際化程序中的“主力軍”，在熊貓債和點心債的外國發行人中均佔據主導地位。 德銀中國行長尹楨在接受記者採訪時表示，當美元和歐元等傳統的全球主要融資貨幣處於相對高息週期時，人民幣不僅具備了極大的融資成本優勢，而且在全球去美元化、新興市場加大本土貨幣使用的宏觀背景下，成為了極具吸引力的替代選擇。 德銀援引萬得資料稱，今年上半年熊貓債發行量同比增長超過60%，結構性增長表明其吸引力已不再侷限於融資成本優勢。 對跨國企業而言，熊貓債正從“戰術選項”變為“戰略組成部分”。包括大眾、賓士、寶馬等汽車製造商，以及巴斯夫、拜耳和漢高等化工企業，近年來多次登陸熊貓債市場。鑑於上述歐洲企業在中國擁有龐大的在岸業務和營收，使用本幣進行融資，能夠天然地對沖其人民幣收入敞口帶來的外匯風險。 與此同時，作為核心的離岸人民幣市場，點心債市場正展現出高度的成熟性。萬得資料顯示，截至今年上半年，點心債發行總規模已突破8000億元，同比增長近50%。德銀報告稱，點心債發行主體日益多元化，境外發行人佔比已攀升至 25%以上。在境內發行人中，科技、IT 等“新經濟”行業已取代傳統行業，成為新的發行主力，以進一步滿足其在人工智慧、雲端計算等領域的資本支出需求。 （第一財經）

【習近平會見柬埔寨首相洪瑪奈】7月17日下午，國家主席習近平在上海西郊賓館會見來華出席2026世界人工智慧大會暨人工智慧全球治理高階別會議並進行工作訪問的柬埔寨首相洪瑪奈。

習近平指出，中柬兩國同舟共濟、守望相助，鐵桿友誼歷經了歲月洗禮和風雨考驗。歷史證明，中柬友好不是一時一事之交，而是穿越風雲變幻、世代相傳的共識，是雙方共同的寶貴財富。雙方要傳承兩國老一輩領導人締造的鐵桿友誼，增進戰略互信，密切團結協作，充實“鑽石六邊”合作內涵，推動新時代全天候中柬命運共同體建設不斷邁向更高水平，更好造福兩國人民。

習近平強調，中國共產黨在管黨治黨、興黨強黨的偉大實踐中形成新時代黨建思想，將持續凝聚中國式現代化建設的磅礴偉力。今年是柬埔寨人民黨成立75週年，中方願同柬方加強治黨治國經驗交流，堅定走好新時代中柬友好合作之路。雙方要發揮中柬政府間協調委員會作用，加快“工業發展走廊”和“魚米走廊”建設，最佳化電力、農業等傳統產業，培育人工智慧、數字經濟等新興產業，統籌推進有利於柬埔寨國計民生的基礎設施建設專案。要用好中柬外交、國防、公安“3+3”戰略對話機制，聯手嚴打製假走私、網賭電詐等跨境犯罪，維護好共同利益和安全。中方願同柬方共同推動落實四大全球倡議，推動構建更加公正合理的全球治理體系。鞏固柬泰停火態勢、逐步建立互信符合兩國的根本和長遠利益，中方支援柬泰雙方透過對話談判找到解決方案，願繼續為此發揮積極作用。

洪瑪奈表示，祝賀中國成功舉辦2026世界人工智慧大會，習近平主席提出的重要理念和主張對於人工智慧全球治理具有重要意義。柬中高層交往密切，各領域合作成果豐碩，感謝中方長期以來為柬埔寨經濟社會發展提供的無私支援和幫助。無論國際形勢如何變化，柬方密切對華友好合作的政策決不會改變，將積極致力於共建柬中命運共同體。柬方堅定奉行一箇中國原則，願同中方弘揚傳統友誼，落實好兩國領導人達成的重要共識，深化政治互信，擴大兩國貿易，開展基礎設施等重大專案合作，加大力度打擊電信詐騙，推動兩國關係發展取得更多成果。柬方感謝中方積極勸和促談，推動和平解決柬泰邊境爭端，柬方將堅持對話談判、緩和局勢。

蔡奇、丁薛祥、王毅、陳吉寧等參加會見。（央視新聞）

### 2026-07-17 18:42:29  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Arabica Coffee Futures at Over 1-Week Low — Arabica coffee futures traded around $3.10 per pound, consolidating near their lowest level in more than a week after a sharp rally and recent volatility driven by weather-related supply concerns. Dealers said the harvest in top coffee grower Brazil had been gathering pace, though lagging last year's pace, reinforcing expectations of greater supply in the short term. Still, market participants continued to monitor bean quality following delays caused by heavy rains in June and early July. Global stocks remain limited and any delay in the arrival of the Brazilian harvest or signs of reduced coffee quality will have a direct impact on international prices. At the same time, buyers and sellers continue to adopt a cautious stance, which reduces the volume of transactions in the physical market.

### 2026-07-17 18:42:07  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

US 10-Year Yield Pulls Back — The yield on the 10-year US Treasury note fell to below 4.55% from the two-month high of 4.62% on July 13th, as softer inflation data pushed back expectations of a Federal Reserve rate hike. Both consumer and producer prices were below expectations in June, suggesting that the pullback in wholesale fuel costs were transmitted to the economy at a greater magnitude than forecast. Fixed-income assets were also supported by new geopolitical uncertainty after US President Trump claimed China compromised US presidential elections in 2020, risking the truce between both countries since the exchange of tariff hikes last year. Still, pro-inflationary risks lingered as strikes between the US and Iran were extended and commercial vessels refrained from crossing the Strait of Hormuz at the elevated traffic volumes from June. Rate futures suggested that over two thirds of the market are positioned for a Fed hike by the end of the year, although this month's decision is expected to be a hold.

### 2026-07-17 18:39:44  #Trading Economics #Economy #Portugal #Current Account #Importance 1

Portugal Current Account Deficit Widens in May — Portugal's current account deficit widened to €632.82 million in May 2026, from €445.25 million in the same month a year earlier. The deterioration was primarily driven by a 4.2% decline in the services account surplus to €2.71 billion, reflecting lower receipts from technical trade-related services and other business services. The goods account deficit also widened, rising 4.7% to €2.57 billion, as import growth (4.2%) outpaced export growth (4.0%), despite tourism revenues increasing to €2.74 billion. Meanwhile, the primary income deficit narrowed 0.6% to €1.27 billion, while the secondary income surplus increased 8.6% to €493 million, supported by higher reinsurance claims received from abroad related to compensation for damages caused by the storms that affected Portugal during the first months of the year.

【覓蜂科技姚卯青：世界模型需要能預測下一步狀態 目前存在兩大問題】7月17日下午，在世界人工智慧大會（WAIC 2026）主論壇“從虛擬到現實：世界模型如何驅動具身智慧”討論環節，智元合夥人、高階副總裁、具身業務部總裁、覓蜂科技董事長兼CEO姚卯青表示，世界模型的本質是“能夠理解物理世界執行規律的AI系統”，其中最重要的功能是能夠預測世界的下一步狀態。今年世界模型雖然火爆，但大部分工作仍停留在視覺生成層面，主要基於影片生成類預訓練模型做後訓練。”姚卯青表示，當前世界模型訓練中存在兩大問題，一是資料型別的錯配，網際網路影片的資料分佈與機器人真正需要的物理世界資料存在本質差異；二是資料規模的不足。

### 2026-07-17 18:26:23  #宏觀

【《資產管理 資料資產分類與程式碼》、《資產管理 資料資產登記指南》國家標準釋出】
近日，國家標準化管理委員會正式釋出《資產管理 資料資產分類與程式碼》（GB/T 47949-2026）、《資產管理 資料資產登記指南》（GB/T 47950-2026）（以下簡稱兩項標準）。兩項標準由財政部組織起草，為推進資料資產嵌入資產管理資訊化流程，規範有序開展資料資產管理提供了操作指引，有利於激發資料資產潛能，推動數字經濟高質量發展。

一是對資料資產進行分類並編碼。《資產管理 資料資產分類與程式碼》遵循《固定資產等資產基礎分類與程式碼》（GB/T 14885-2022）的分類與編碼原則，聚焦資料要素基礎屬性，將資料資產分為結構化資料、半結構化資料、非結構化資料三類，以及16項具體細類，並按大類、中類、小類逐級細分，兼顧分類粒度與實用性。同時，預留分類程式碼拓展空間，適應資料資產型別的快速演進和創新迭代。 

二是為資料資產登記工作提供操作指引。《資產管理 資料資產登記指南》確立了資料資產登記的總體原則，明確了資料資產登記實施方、登記內容、登記流程和要求，重點圍繞資料資產初始、變更和登出登記三個環節，提出資產臺賬登記和會計賬簿登記的具體要求，形成崗位職責清晰、流程規範的資料資產登記管理體系。 （財政部）

### 2026-07-17 18:19:02  #Trading Economics #Economy #Albania #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

Albania Trade Deficit Widens in June — Albania’s trade deficit widened to ALL 48.0 billion in June 2026 from ALL 45.8 billion in the same month a year earlier. Imports rose 9.1% year-on-year to ALL 83.5 billion, supported by higher purchases of food, beverages, and tobacco (11.6%), chemicals and plastic products (17.8%), machinery, equipment, and spare parts (3.4%), and minerals, fuels, and electricity (24.4%). Among key trading partners, imports increased from Italy (24.9%), China (11.5%), Greece (6.9%), and Germany (3.4%). Meanwhile, exports climbed 13.6% to ALL 35.4 billion, driven by stronger shipments of minerals, fuels, and electricity (31.0%), food, beverages, and tobacco (8.5%), construction materials and metals (29.1%), and machinery, equipment, and spare parts (30.1%). Exports rose the most to Kosovo (38.5%), Greece (24.2%), and Italy (7.6%). During the January–June period, the country’s trade gap totaled ALL 256.4 billion, up from ALL 249.1 billion in the corresponding period of last year.

【萊茵河水位下降 客船擱淺輪渡暫停】萊茵河水位下降波及德國西部多地，波恩市一艘客船16日擱淺，萊沃庫森市輪渡服務暫停。波恩市應急部門在一份宣告中說，這艘船載有75名乘客及40名船員，16日上午靠岸時擱淺在沙灘上。拖船當天下午將其拖離，其他船隻仍可減速通行。波恩以北萊沃庫森市16日起暫停萊茵河渡輪服務，將根據水位變化決定重啟渡輪時間。萊沃庫森市政府說，萊茵河水位本週末或小幅上漲。杜伊斯堡港港口運營商發言人安德烈亞斯·巴特爾稱，萊茵河水位下降導致船隻裝載量下降，貨運運力下降，供應鏈運輸成本增加。最近數月來，罕見熱浪席捲歐洲多地。德國氣象局統計資料顯示，今年6月是德國有記錄以來第二熱的6月。萊茵河流量處於低位，地下水也處於較低水平。（央視新聞）

【歐盟提議自2031年起放緩碳排放市場配額削減速度】歐盟委員會透過一項提案，計劃改革2030年後的歐盟碳排放交易體系（EU ETS），包括放緩碳配額（即排放上限）的年度削減速度，並延長免費配額的發放期限。歐盟提案設定：2031–2035年，碳排放上限的年度削減率為3.7%；2036年起降至1.7%。對受碳邊境調節機制（CBAM）覆蓋的行業，其免費配額的逐步退出期限將延長至2037年。2030年後，免費配額的發放將附加條件：企業鬚髮布經董事會批准的脫碳投資計劃，每年可獲得80%的免費配額；剩餘20%在投資計劃實施後發放。

【European Commission proposes revisions to EU Emissions Trading System】The European Commission proposed lowering the ETS linear reduction factor to 3.1% for 2031–35 and 1.7% from 2036 onward, down from the current 4.3%. It would cut the Market Stability Reserve intake rate to 12% after 2030 (vs. 24% now), allow international carbon offset credits to cover 2% of emissions reductions by ETS-covered sectors, add 250 million tonnes of domestic carbon removals into the ETS, and frontload 80% of free allowances to industry with the remaining 20% issued only after verified decarbonisation investments.

### 2026-07-17 18:03:42  #行業

【稅務總局印發公告進一步最佳化企業重組所得稅處理徵管規定】近日，為支援企業併購重組，國家稅務總局制發了《關於企業重組業務所得稅處理有關征管問題的公告》（簡稱《公告》），明確自2026年1月1日起，將達成特殊性稅務處理一致性意見的居民企業股東持股比例由100%放寬至50%，適當擴大重組當事各方主體型別，並規範執行口徑，為企業重組營造良好政策環境。據瞭解，此前政策要求包括所有股東在內的重組當事各方均達成意見後，才能適用特殊性稅務處理。近年來，上市公司吸收合併等重組業務逐漸增多，希望適用特殊性稅務處理的訴求也越來越多。但上市公司股東眾多，所有股東均一致同意的難度較大，難以適用特殊性稅務處理，此外，上市公司股東還包括合夥企業、契約型資管產品、非居民企業等型別，也會影響居民企業當事方適用特殊性稅務處理。稅務總局所得稅司有關負責人介紹，《公告》堅持問題導向，聚焦企業重組希望便捷適用特殊性稅務處理的需要，進一步最佳化企業重組企業所得稅處理徵管口徑，及時回應各方訴求。這是稅務機關牢固樹立和踐行正確政績觀，助力經濟高質量發展的重要舉措，既有利於進一步推動產業轉型升級，提升資源配置效率，又有利於提高政策確定性，降低納稅人遵從成本。《公告》指出，在合併、分立業務中，持股比例超過50%以上的居民企業股東與合併（分立）、被合併（被分立）企業達成一致，且符合其他條件的，可對達成一致的部分適用特殊性稅務處理，即在當期暫不確認所得，緩解上市公司當期繳稅壓力，降低財務成本，助力資源有效整合；其餘部分適用一般性稅務處理，並可選擇簡便計稅方法。合併、分立業務中，如涉及自然人、合夥企業、契約型資管產品以及非居民企業股東，這些股東本身需按現行規定進行所得稅處理,但不影響居民企業股東適用特殊性稅務處理。《公告》在適度最佳化徵管口徑的同時，遵循特殊性稅務處理的基本原理，採取必要措施保證重組權益連續性和經營連續性。比如，要求持股5%以上的居民企業股東及前十大居民企業股東需對適用特殊性稅務處理達成一致，上述大股東在12個月以內轉讓股權的，不再符合特殊性稅務處理條件。上述負責人表示，稅務部門將持續落實好企業重組稅收政策，引導企業合法合規適用優惠政策，在助力企業健康發展的同時，營造法治公平的營商環境。(稅務總局）

---

【財政部、應急管理部緊急預撥5000萬元中央自然災害救災資金支援重慶做好彭水烏江山體崩塌應急搶險救災工作】7月17日上午，重慶彭水烏江沿岸發生山體崩塌，造成多棟居民樓被掩埋、人員被困。地質災害發生後，應急管理部啟動國家地質災害二級應急響應，國家防災減災救災委員會啟動國家救災四級應急響應。為貫徹落實習近平總書記關於防災減災救災工作的重要指示精神，當日，財政部、應急管理部緊急預撥5000萬元中央自然災害救災資金，支援重慶開展山體崩塌應急搶險救援和受災群眾救助工作，重點做好人員搜救、排危除險等應急處置、開展次生災害隱患排查、受災群眾轉移安置以及倒損民房修復等，最大限度減少災害損失和人員傷亡。 財政部要求重慶市財政局抓緊將資金撥付災區，加強救災資金監管，確保救災資金全部用於災區和受災群眾，切實保障人民群眾生命財產安全。 （財政部）

### 2026-07-17 18:00:18  #公司 #市場 #國際

【美國企業高管大舉減持股票 拋售規模接近峰值水平】美國企業高管拋售股票的規模創20多年來次高水平。一些投資者將這種行為視為典型的警示訊號，表明最瞭解企業狀況的人員對當前市場持謹慎態度。根據EPFR Global Market Intelligence，2026年上半年，企業內部人員累計拋售價值776億美元的股票，較上年同期增長20%。此前，只有2021年時拋售規模更大，當時市場受到疫情期間大規模刺激措施推動，流動性極為充裕。包括Winston Chua在內的EPFR分析師寫道，“企業內部人士的交易活動表明，高管並不急於在當前估值水平下增加敞口。”與此同時，企業內部人士買入股票的規模依然低迷。上半年他們累計買入69億美元股票，僅略高於上年同期創下的67億美元七年低點。EPFR分析師團隊表示，“即便股市持續上漲，企業內部人士仍不願增加個人股票持倉。”標普500指數今年以來已上漲10%，有望連續第四年錄得兩位數漲幅。不過，近期交易員開始緊張起來，擔心晶片股漲勢過快過猛，以及人工智慧領域的支出出現過熱跡象。此外，隨著更多大型人工智慧公司尋求上市，市場也擔心股票供應增加可能對股市形成壓力。

【古特雷斯：人工智慧可以成為21世紀人類最大機遇 感謝中方促成本次盛會】7月17日上午，國家主席習近平在上海世界會客廳出席2026世界人工智慧大會暨人工智慧全球治理高階別會議開幕式並發表主旨講話。哈薩克總統託卡耶夫、柬埔寨首相洪瑪奈、泰國總理阿努廷、聯合國秘書長古特雷斯分別致辭。聯合國秘書長古特雷斯說：“各位閣下，女士們，先生們，人工智慧可以成為21世紀人類最大的機遇。本屆大會的主題也昭示我們，更光明的未來有賴於夥伴關係。感謝中方秉持這一理念促成本次盛會。習主席2023年提出《全球人工智慧治理倡議》，充分彰顯了中國的擔當。世界人工智慧合作組織的成立也是其順理成章的結果。在關乎人類共同未來的諸多領域中，人工智慧在可持續發展領域的應用前景尤為廣闊。我們面臨的挑戰是確保人工智慧不止追求共同進步，更要實現廣泛包容。中國在其中肩負著至關重要的作用，世界各國同樣責無旁貸。”（央視新聞）

### 2026-07-17 17:52:14  #Trading Economics #Economy #Slovakia #Current Account #Importance 1

Slovakia's Current Account Swings to Surplus in May — Slovakia's current account posted a surplus of €109.8 million in May 2026, compared with a €156.4 million deficit in the same month a year earlier. This marks its first monthly surplus in nearly three years after a prolonged period of consecutive deficits, primarily driven by a 90.4% year-on-year surge in the goods account to €447.2 million, the highest level in more than a decade. The services account also strengthened, with its surplus increasing 28.7% to €52.5 million. The secondary income account remained a drag on the current account, narrowing by 14.6% to €107.6 million. Meanwhile, the primary income account deteriorated, with its deficit widening 7.8% to €282.2 million. For the first five months of 2026, Slovakia's cumulative current account deficit narrowed to €843.3 million.

【美國多州環孢子蟲病感染或與墨西哥進口球生菜有關】美國食品和藥物管理局16日表示，針對美國5個州報告的環孢子蟲病感染病例調查顯示，感染源可能與美國一家快餐連鎖店部分門店從墨西哥進口的球生菜有關。美藥管局當天釋出的調查報告說，此次調查涉及印第安納州、肯塔基州、密歇根州、俄亥俄州和西弗吉尼亞州。目前調查仍在進行，不排除未來擴大至更多州。美國疾病控制和預防中心資料顯示，截至目前，上述5州共報告1644例環孢子蟲病感染病例，這些患者說，發病前曾在美國快餐連鎖店塔可鐘的門店用餐。患者發病時間介於今年5月13日至7月13日之間，其中94人住院，尚無死亡病例報告。密歇根州公共衛生部門對該州190例曾在塔可鍾用餐的病例開展的流行病學調查發現，90%的被調查者表示曾食用過球生菜。報告說，供應鏈追溯調查顯示，患者發病前在這些門店食用的球生菜均來自墨西哥同一家供應商。（新華社）

### 2026-07-17 17:30:16  #MKT News #Commodities #Energy #WTI #Impact bullish #Brent #XAUUSD

【US-Iran attacks escalate; which side most urgently needs a ceasefire?】US-Iran tit‑for‑tat strikes have accelerated, keeping oil markets, regional risk and US munitions inventories in focus. Iran faces deep economic strain from decades of US sanctions and recent fighting: per‑capita GDP has fallen from about $8,000 to $5,000 and oil exports have dropped from roughly 2.2m bpd to 1.5m bpd. A June memorandum briefly eased restrictions — 60‑day waivers and asset unfreezing pushed the rial up ~15% — but the US has reimposed sanctions this week. CSIS analysis says Iran has taken heavy military attrition: as of April 1 missile stocks down ~30% and drone stocks down ~60%, with significant damage to naval infrastructure and weapons production sites; strikes have continued, including recent attacks on Greater Tunb island. Oil jumped about 12% after the latest strikes amid renewed fears for Strait of Hormuz transit; roughly one‑fifth of global oil flows use the strait. US domestic politics and costs matter: US average gasoline rose from $2.98/gal pre‑conflict to a May peak of $4.63/gal, and a YouGov poll shows 57% of Americans view participation as wrong ahead of November midterms. US munitions consumption is also a constraint — CSIS reports four of seven main combat munitions were more than half expended in the first phase; replenishment under industrial measures would take months to years — and analysts warn key interceptors and cruise missiles are being consumed rapidly. Iran appears resilient: analysts say leadership views the fight as existential, drone production reportedly resumed after April ceasefire and could replenish stocks within months. Regionally, Gulf states are strengthening data‑sharing and early‑warning coordination. Market takeaway: elevated oil risk premium, potential for protracted disruption to regional supply, and near‑term pressure on US military inventories and political capital.

【ChatGPT核心貢獻者、亮源新創創始人姜旭：找到適合模型的產品和場景比訓練模型更難】7月17日，在2026世界人工智慧大會（WAIC）期間，亮源新創創始人兼CEO、ChatGPT核心貢獻者姜旭提出了具身智慧發展的三大正規化：規模化預訓練（Scalable Pre-training）、規模化對齊（Scalable Alignment）和規模化部署（Scalable Deployment）。他表示，大模型的訓練路徑已經越來越清晰，但真正找到適合模型能力的產品形態和商業場景，並不比訓練模型本身更容易。OpenAI在ChatGPT成功之前，也經歷了大量產品探索，其中很多都沒有成功。對於具身智慧公司而言，更現實的策略，是保持開放心態，持續探索豐富的應用場景，而不是過早假設唯一正確的商業化方向。

【瑞銀維持看漲標普500：看好盈利前景 預計今年AI資本支出激增68%】瑞銀全球財富管理部門對標普500指數年末7900點的目標價保持不變。本次新增的核心內容，是這家瑞士銀行闡述了推動大盤開啟下一輪上行行情的核心驅動力。 該行認為，能夠支撐美股在今年收官階段強勢收官的，將是強勁的現金流、盈利增長以及企業自身經營落地能力，而非泛泛而談的人工智慧題材熱度。瑞銀對標普500指數的看漲預測，首要依託於大幅上調指數整體盈利預期。 該行將該指數2026年每股收益預期從310美元上調至335美元，對應年度盈利增速從此前預測的11%提升至近20%。另外瑞銀預計，2026年全球人工智慧相關資本支出將激增68%，總額約8200億美元；2027年該項支出將繼續增長21%，規模接近1萬億美元。當前半導體供應緊張、晶片租賃價格上漲，疊加企業持續融資募資，短期內行業幾乎不可能縮減資本開支。

【花旗：瀾起科技強勁業績及股份回購有助穩定股價波動，維持“買入”評級】花旗發表研報指，瀾起科技公佈強勁的2026年上半年初步業績，第二季淨利潤介乎10.53億至12.53億元，同比增長66%至98%，分別高於市場普遍預期及該行預期達37%和42%，主要受到更高收入規模、毛利率擴張及投資收益推動。期內收入同比增長33%至18.74億元，超市場及該行預期分別6%及11%。其中互連晶片收入同比增長28%至16.94億元。DDR5 RCD出貨量持續增長，且Gen 3及Gen 4佔比進一步擴大。該行提到，雖然韓國檢察官因懷疑價格壟斷串謀而突擊搜查相關辦公室的訊息導致股價下挫，且調查仍在進行並可能引致股價進一步波動，但該行認為強勁的2026年業績預告，加上管理層公佈的3億至6億元A股股份回購計劃，應有助於穩定股價。花旗表示，瀾起科技H股為國際投資者參與中國及全球AI資料中心擴充套件提供了罕有機會，其強烈的AI主題獲得國際投資者青睞，導致H股較A股出現溢價。該行對其港股目標價設於305港元，對應預測2027年市盈率66倍，維持“買入”評級。

【Euro-area June inflation eases, ECB keeps policy flexibility】Euro-area inflation cooled slightly in June, matching the flash estimate: headline MoM -0.1%, led by energy disinflation (-1.8%). Energy YoY inflation fell to 8.5% from 10.8% in May. Food inflation slowed to 1.5% YoY (from 1.9%) and services to 3.2% (from 3.5%), leaving core inflation on a downward path. The print reduces near-term upside inflation risk and gives the ECB greater flexibility to wait before adjusting policy over the summer. Key risk: renewed Middle East tensions could lift oil and gas prices, raising summer price pressure and increasing the chance of second-round effects.

### 2026-07-17 17:13:41  #Trading Economics #Economy #Malta #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Malta Annual Inflation Eases to 2025-Lows — Malta's annual inflation rate eased to 2.0% in June 2026, marking the lowest level since February 2025, down from 2.1% in the previous month. The slowdown was mainly supported by easing price growth in food (1.8% vs 2.0%) and transport (2.6% vs 2.9%), followed by recreation, sport and culture (3.4% vs 3.9%), health (3.0% vs 3.1%) and restaurants and accommodation services (3.0% vs 3.6%). Meanwhile, deflation eased for clothing and footwear (-3.9% vs -4.3%) and information and communication (-1.6% vs -2.5%). In contrast, price growth accelerated for housing and utilities (2.8% vs 2.5%), furnishings (2.6% vs 2.4%), alcoholic beverages, tobacco and narcotics (1.6% vs 1.5%) and education services (3.2% vs 3.1%). Costs in insurance and financial services rebounded, increased 0.3% following a 0.2% drop. On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose 1.1%, easing from 1.4% in the previous month.

### 2026-07-17 17:13:10  #Trading Economics #Economy #Euro Area #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Eurozone Inflation Confirmed at Four-Month Low in June — Eurozone annual inflation was confirmed at 2.8% in June 2026, down from 3.2% in May and marking its lowest level since February, before the Iran war disrupted energy supplies and drove oil prices higher. While inflation continued to ease, it remained above the European Central Bank's 2.0% target. Energy inflation slowed sharply to 8.5% from 10.8%, while price growth also moderated for services, non-energy industrial goods, and food, alcohol, and tobacco. Core inflation, which excludes energy and food, eased to 2.4% from 2.6%. Among the bloc's largest economies, inflation slowed in Germany, France, Italy, and the Netherlands, while remaining unchanged at 3.6% in Spain.

【中信建投黃文濤：矽基延續高景氣，內需待政策接力】中信建投黃文濤釋出研報稱，6月資料表明，增長斜率有所改善，結構分化尚未扭轉。K型分化加深體現為四個層面：生產強於需求，外需強於內需，高技術強於傳統部門，短期融資強於長期融資。近期政策思路與當前矛盾的匹配度提高。政策主線聚焦於提升消費能力，盤活城市存量，建設現代基礎設施。城市更新和“六張網”有望託底投資，消費與就業政策則決定內需修復的持續性。其中，高技術製造、現代服務和相關外需繼續走強。工業和服務業生產同步加快。6月製造業PMI升至50.3%。上半年裝備製造業和高技術製造業增加值分別增長9.3%和13.3%。工業機器人、鋰離子電池和3D列印裝置保持高增，資訊通訊行業增長10.7%。同時，出口結構也進一步向技術產品傾斜。機電產品出口增長20.1%，佔出口總額超過六成。矽基生產力仍是產出、利潤和外需的主要增量。

### 2026-07-17 17:08:50  #市場 #國際

【瑞銀資產管理高管看空美債 認為德債是極佳的防禦性配置選擇】瑞銀資產管理的Kevin Zhao計劃做空美國國債，押注美國經濟表現強勁將削弱政府債券作為避險資產的吸引力。“我們仍希望逢高賣出，”這位瑞銀資管的全球主權及貨幣基金主管在接受採訪時稱。“美國仍然處於最有利的位置。它沒有受到能源衝擊影響，並且仍是人工智慧(AI)投資熱潮的最大受益者。”另一方面，他表示德國國債是極佳的防禦性配置選擇。他認為能源衝擊將對歐洲經濟增長造成更大打擊，而該地區從AI投資中獲益的程度相對有限，這提升了德債的吸引力。債券經理們如今越來越擔心，美國通脹加速的局面正在固化，侵蝕投資回報並導致利率居高不下。Zhao表示，近期美伊衝突升級導致油價飆升，對歐洲增長構成的威脅大於對通脹的推升作用。“我們仍看好歐洲債券，因為歐洲經濟並未從AI投資熱潮中受益多少，而能源成本上升正嚴重影響其經濟增長，”他指出。與歐洲相仿，亞洲也依賴能源進口。但Zhao表示，這種拖累將被AI領域的投資所抵消。他表示，如果較長期德國國債收益率再漲10個基點，“我們就會買入”。10年期德債收益率目前在3.12%，距離5月觸及的15年高點3.20%已不遠。Zhao指出，明年的法國和義大利大選將進一步提升德債吸引力，因為選舉將推高政治風險。

### 2026-07-17 17:06:41  #國際

【美伊戰事影響進一步外溢 巴基斯坦劃出“紅線”】
自美伊戰事爆發以來，巴基斯坦發揮了重要斡旋作用。然而，訊息人士指出，沙烏地阿拉伯與葉門胡塞武裝日前的互襲事件，令巴基斯坦面臨捲入衝突的巨大風險，巴基斯坦方面已向伊朗方面鄭重表態，對沙特的襲擊就是對巴方的襲擊，這是巴方“紅線”。
胡塞武裝是伊朗的盟友。路透社16日援引巴方官員的話報道，巴政府和軍方領導人已把其“紅線”立場傳遞至伊朗最高層。
報道說，自美伊戰事爆發以來，巴基斯坦很少如此接近被迫“選邊站”。巴基斯坦安全分析師穆罕默德·阿米爾·拉納說，巴方沒想到緊張局勢會“驟然升級”。
依據沙特和巴基斯坦2025年9月簽署的共同戰略防禦協議，第三方針對兩國任何一國的攻擊將被視為“對兩國的侵略”。本月13日，胡塞武裝以沙特空襲其控制的葉門首都薩那國際機場為由，向沙特西南部的艾卜哈國際機場發射導彈和無人機。依照美國媒體說法，這是雙方2022年以來最為嚴重的衝突。
截至目前，上述衝突被視為“單一事件”。外界擔心，一旦沙特與胡塞武裝戰火重燃，地區緊張局勢將升級，美伊戰事影響將進一步外溢。
巴基斯坦外交部發言人塔希爾·安德拉比16日說，巴方呼籲各方保持最大剋制，透過對話和外交手段解決問題。
據路透社報道，沙特和巴基斯坦簽署共同戰略防禦協議，反映出海灣國家對美國提供所謂“安全保障”愈發心存疑慮，看好巴基斯坦等國為替代選項。
另據路透社16日援引訊息人士的話報道，伊朗告訴胡塞武裝，若美國襲擊伊朗電力網路，胡塞武裝就封鎖曼德海峽。伊朗官方未對此作出回應。 
報道說，胡塞武裝已完成相應準備，在曼德海峽附近部署導彈和無人機。有訊息人士指出，封鎖曼德海峽“不難”，要擾亂該水域的航運“甚至不需要複雜的導彈”。
曼德海峽連線紅海和亞丁灣，是溝通大西洋、地中海和印度洋的“咽喉”，被稱為連線歐亞非三大洲的“水上走廊”。在霍爾木茲海峽航運已因美伊戰事嚴重受阻的情況下，曼德海峽如果也被阻塞，將再度衝擊全球能源供應。
為繞開受阻的霍爾木茲海峽，沙烏地阿拉伯國家石油公司（沙特阿美）利用一條橫貫東西的輸油管道，把沙特東部產油區生產的原油輸送到紅海沿岸的延布港，再經曼德海峽出口。專業機構提供的資料顯示，延布港最近日均原油裝運量已接近峰值，為去年同期的4倍多。
巴基斯坦高度依賴中東油氣進口，其國內能源供應已因美伊戰事受到影響。路透社援引巴方人士的話報道：“戰事結束符合各方最大利益。”（新華社）

### 2026-07-17 17:04:58  #Trading Economics #Economy #Bulgaria #Current Account #Importance 1

Bulgaria Current Account Deficit Widens in May — Bulgaria’s current account deficit widened sharply to EUR 597.8 million in May 2026 from EUR 88.2 million in the corresponding month of the previous year. The goods account deficit increased to EUR 782.6 million from EUR 419.2 million, while the services account surplus narrowed to EUR 556.9 million from EUR 634.1 million, mainly due to weaker manufacturing services, which stagnated (vs EUR 10.4 million), and transportation services (EUR 190.5 million vs EUR 201.2 million). Meanwhile, the primary income deficit widened slightly to EUR 480.1 million from EUR 479.4 million a year earlier. The secondary income surplus also declined by EUR 68.3 million to EUR 108 million.

### 2026-07-17 17:00:31  #Trading Economics #Economy #Hong Kong #Business Confidence #Importance 1

Hong Kong Business Morale Improves Slightly in Q3 — Business confidence in Hong Kong edged up to -4 in the third quarter of 2026 from -5 in the previous quarter. The share of respondents expecting a better business condition declined to 10% from 11% in Q2 2026, while those anticipating worse conditions eased slightly to 14% from 16%. Confidence strengthened in manufacturing (5 vs 0 in Q2) and sentiment also improved, though remained negative, in accommodation and food services (-9 vs -35), construction (-6 vs -21), and transportation, storage and courier services (-15 vs -24), information and communications (-7 vs -16), and retail (-2 vs -3). Confidence in import/export trade and wholesale was unchanged at -9. However, optimism eased in financing and insurance (10 vs 12), while sentiment deteriorated sharply in real estate (-11 vs 9) and professional and business services (-10 vs 2).

### 2026-07-17 16:53:13  #Trading Economics #Economy #Hong Kong #Unemployment Rate #Importance 1

Hong Kong Unemployment Rate Steady — Hong Kong’s seasonally adjusted unemployment rate held steady at 3.7% in the three months ending June 2026, remaining at its lowest level since the period ending August 2025. The number of unemployed people declined by 1,400 month-on-month to 139,700, while total employment increased by 6,800 to 3.6 million. However, labor market conditions among younger workers weakened, with the unemployment rate for those aged 20 to 29 rising to 7.5% from 7%, the highest since the three months ending November 2025. The underemployment rate also edged up to 1.6% from 1.5%. Meanwhile, the labor force participation rate inched higher to 56.2% from 56.1% in the previous period.

### 2026-07-17 16:52:42  #Trading Economics #Economy #Kazakhstan #Industrial Production #Importance 1

Kazakhstan Industrial Activity Expands — Kazakhstan’s industrial production rose 8.2% year-on-year in June 2026, accelerating from 4.7% in the previous month. The largest jump was recorded in the manufacturing sector, surging 13.7% from 5.2% in the previous month, followed by the supply of electricity, gas, steam, hot water and air conditioning, edging up to 16.9% from 16%. Meanwhile, mining quarrying output slowed to 1.8% from 3%, largely due to a decrease in production of crude oil and natural gas. Activity in water supply, water drainage, waste collection, treatment and disposal, pollution abatement services contracted 1.3% after a 2.9% increase in the previous month. For the first half of the year, industrial production rose 3.5% compared to the same period a year earlier.

### 2026-07-17 16:42:43  #國際

【以色列議會解散 內塔尼亞胡政府轉為看守政府】以色列議會於17日凌晨解散，新一屆議會選舉將在10月27日舉行。以色列總理內塔尼亞胡領導的政府在新政府成立前將作為看守政府繼續履職。根據以色列法律，議會選舉原則上每4年舉行一次。以色列媒體報道說，此次選舉是以色列38年來首次按法定任期而非提前舉行的議會選舉。根據規定，從議會解散起至新政府成立前，現政府將轉為看守政府，原則上不得推動實施重大政策或具有爭議性的立法，僅可處理獲得廣泛支援的法案、日常行政事務及國家安全所需事項。以色列媒體近期公佈的多項民調顯示，內塔尼亞胡領導的執政聯盟預計將在擁有120個席位的議會中獲得約50席，低於組建政府所需的61席，按目前聯盟格局難以繼續執政。（新華社）

【Shanghai issues measures to advance AI+manufacturing】Shanghai Municipal Commission of Economy and Informatization issued measures to accelerate AI+manufacturing, offering targeted grants and subsidies to speed R&D and deployment. Up to 20 mln yuan in support for breakthroughs in core technologies including industrial vertical large models, AI coding models, physical AI, industrial agents, industrial software and industrial internet — with specific aims such as knowledge-graph fusion and text-to-3D parts design. Up to 10 mln yuan for integrated security solutions for industrial models and agents. To lower intelligent-input costs, the city encourages industrial cloud platforms to offer low-code agent development, free trials, token experience vouchers and preferential compute plans; subsidies of up to 40 mln yuan for leasing non-affiliated compute resources for industrial model/agent R&D. Subsidies up to 5 mln yuan each for invoking or privately deploying third‑party models and for purchasing high-quality training corpora for industrial model and agent development.

【US strikes destroy five Hormozgan bridges, severing Bandar Abbas logistics】US strikes destroyed five bridges in Hormozgan province, cutting road links between Bandar Abbas and surrounding districts. Bandar Abbas, Iran’s primary southern naval logistics hub, supports IRGC, coastal defense, missile, engineering units and transport of military supplies. Damage to transport nodes may delay fuel, ammunition, engineering gear, spare parts and other military materiel, lowering logistics efficiency and degrading operational execution. Rapid deployment and mobility of anti-ship cruise missiles, coastal defense systems, ballistic missile launchers and UAVs could be impaired; with fewer road corridors formerly dispersed assets may become easier to detect and track by ISR. The Hormozgan road network also underpins Iran’s monitoring and reinforcement of Strait of Hormuz coastal bases; damaged routes will hinder troop and resupply movements, radar sustainment, launcher repairs and the maintenance of deterrent/threat capabilities against maritime traffic.

### 2026-07-17 16:36:27  #宏觀 #公司

【電池消費稅政策將分步調整】記者7月17日從財政部瞭解到，財政部、海關總署、稅務總局近日釋出公告，明確自2026年9月1日起，分步調整部分電池產品消費稅政策。我國當前對電池徵收4%的消費稅，這是從2015年2月起開始實施的稅收政策。政策同時明確，對包括鋰原電池、鋰離子蓄電池、太陽能電池在內的七類電池免徵消費稅。根據公告，此次政策調整將分類分步對部分電池恢復徵收消費稅。自2026年9月1日起，對無汞原電池、金屬氫化物鎳蓄電池、鋰原電池、鋰離子蓄電池、全釩液流電池按照2%稅率徵收消費稅；自2027年9月1日起，按照4%稅率徵收消費稅。自2027年4月1日起，對光伏電池（又稱“太陽能電池”）按照2%稅率徵收消費稅；自2028年4月1日起，按照4%稅率徵收消費稅。同時，部分新技術電池在一定期限內享受消費稅免稅優惠。根據公告，自2026年9月1日起至2028年12月31日，對鈉離子電池、固態電池、燃料電池及光伏電池中的鈣鈦礦電池、疊層電池、砷化鎵電池免徵消費稅。業內人士稱，總的來看，此次政策調整順應我國電池產業發展形勢變化，有利於更好發揮消費稅調節作用，促進資源節約和環境保護；有利於促進電池行業健康高質量發展，推動行業技術進步和產業升級。（新華社）

【財政部等部門：調整部分電池消費稅政策】為進一步完善消費稅制度，現將電池消費稅政策調整事項公告如下：一、自2026年9月1日起，對無汞原電池、金屬氫化物鎳蓄電池（又稱氫鎳蓄電池或鎳氫蓄電池）、鋰原電池、鋰離子蓄電池、全釩液流電池按照2%稅率徵收消費稅；自2027年9月1日起，對上述電池產品按照4%稅率徵收消費稅。二、自2027年4月1日起，對光伏電池（又稱太陽能電池）按照2%稅率徵收消費稅；自2028年4月1日起，對光伏電池按照4%稅率徵收消費稅。三、自2026年9月1日起至2028年12月31日，對鈉離子電池、固態電池、燃料電池及光伏電池中的鈣鈦礦電池、疊層電池、砷化鎵電池免徵消費稅。四、納稅人生產、委託加工享受上述減免稅政策的電池產品，應當符合該產品的國家標準，對不符合國家標準或沒有國家標準的產品，不得享受上述減免稅政策。

### 2026-07-17 16:24:32  #Trading Economics #Economy #Euro Area #Current Account #Importance 2

Eurozone Current Account Deficit Narrows in May — The Eurozone recorded a current account deficit of €6.19 billion in May 2026, narrowing slightly from a €6.57 billion shortfall a year earlier. The improvement was driven by a sharp reduction in the primary income deficit to €22.44 billion from a record €42.35 billion in May 2025. Meanwhile, the secondary income deficit widened to €13.19 billion from €10.84 billion. The goods surplus narrowed to €9.45 billion, its smallest since April 2023, while the services surplus increased to €19.99 billion from €16.42 billion. For the January–May period, the current account surplus rose to €67.5 billion from €64.37 billion a year earlier.

【US futures fall as semiconductor sell-off accelerates, investors rotate】US equity futures fell as a semiconductor sell-off intensified, prompting investor rotation into other sectors. Nasdaq-100 futures were down more than 2% and S&P 500 futures fell over 1%. Nvidia (NVDA.O) led the "seven megacaps" lower in premarket trading; the Philadelphia Semiconductor Index is approaching bear-market territory and extended Thursday's losses. Despite the S&P 500 closing 0.5% lower on Thursday, 369 constituents rose and 132 fell, signaling still-healthy market breadth. BARCLAYS strategist Venu Krishna said AI-capex enthusiasm is cooling, but semiconductors still materially outperform the broader market while software lags, implying recent rotation is gradual rather than decisive.

Italy Construction Output Rises 2.9% in May — Italy’s construction output rose 2.9% year-on-year in May 2026, easing from an upwardly revised 3.2% increase in the previous period. On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, construction output was unchanged, interrupting the growth recorded in the previous two months (+1.9% in March and +0.8% in April). On average, over the March-to-May period, construction production increased 2.0% from the previous three-month period. In the first five months of 2026, calendar-adjusted construction output rose 2.0%.

Italy Current Account Surplus Shrinks in May — Italy’s current account surplus shrank to EUR 593.9 million in May 2026 from EUR 1,906.9 million in the same month of the previous year. This marked the smallest surplus since a deficit in January, as the goods surplus declined to EUR 5,224.4 million from EUR 6,171.5 million, while the services balance swung to a shortfall of EUR 118.2 million from a surplus of EUR 173.9 million a year earlier. The primary income deficit also widened to EUR 4,034.4 million from EUR 3,746.7 million. Meanwhile, the secondary income gap narrowed to EUR 477.9 million from EUR 691.8 million.

【Euro area current account surplus widens in May as primary income rise offsets narrower trade surplus】ECB data on Friday showed the euro area seasonally adjusted current account surplus widened to €25.1 bln in May from €17.5 bln in April, driven by higher primary income (interest, profits and dividends) that offset a narrower trade surplus. Unadjusted data showed a €6.2 bln current account deficit in May versus a €16.7 bln surplus in April. Over the 12 months to May the current account surplus was 1.7% of GDP, down from 2.0% a year earlier.

### 2026-07-17 16:11:11  #Trading Economics #Markets #Germany #Stock Market #Importance 1

DAX Down for 3rd Day — The DAX 40 fell 0.5% to around 24,800 on Friday, marking the third straight of losses, pressured by a sharp selloff in tech stocks and concerns about escalating tensions in the Middle East. Oil prices remained elevated as the United States and Iran intensified attacks across the Gulf, disrupting oil flows through the Strait of Hormuz and keeping inflation concerns alive. Markets largely expect the ECB to maintain rates steady next week before potentially delivering a cut in September. Chipmaker Infineon Technologies (-5%) and AI infrastructure-linked Siemens Energy (-2.6%) and Hochtief (-2.5%) were among the worst performers. Deutsche Bank and Commerzbank also faced pressure, falling 1.5% and 1.4%, respectively. On the upside, Deutsche Telekom (2.5%), E.ON (1.7%) and SAP (1.6%) stood out. For the week, the index was set to decline over 1%.

### 2026-07-17 16:10:17  #Trading Economics #Markets #Italy #Stock Market #Importance 0

FTSE MIB Falls for Third Session — The FTSE MIB fell to around 52,100 on Friday, extending losses for a third straight session as renewed concerns over elevated AI-related valuations and ongoing tensions between the US and Iran weighed on risk sentiment. Semiconductor firm STMicroelectronics plunged more than 6%, while cable maker Prysmian dropped over 4%, tracking weakness in global technology stocks. Losses also spread across other sectors, with luxury names Moncler and Brunello Cucinelli falling 1.3% and 1.1%, respectively. Financials also weighed on the index, led by heavyweight UniCredit (-1.1%), followed by BPER Banca (-0.9%) and Intesa Sanpaolo (-0.5%). Limiting the downside, utilities outperformed, with Terna (+2.3%), Snam (+2.2%), and Enel (+1.7%) posting solid gains, while Eni rose 1.3% as firmer oil prices supported energy shares. For the week, the index was on track for a second consecutive decline.

【多隊球衣熱銷斷貨 耐克庫存不足錯失世界盃商機】耐克偏偏在美國男足世界盃球衣最該補貨熱銷的關鍵時期庫存告急。如今雖然完成補貨，卻已然錯過了球隊本屆世界盃征程帶來的銷售視窗期。 美國男足在小組賽前兩場接連大勝巴拉圭與澳大利亞，開賽僅兩週，多款美國隊球衣便全部售罄，這足以說明作為球隊贊助商的耐克嚴重低估了這款球衣的市場需求。 耐克緊急加急備貨、向線下門店補發貨品，但補貨送達為時已晚。在美國隊爆冷不敵比利時、早早止步世界盃一週多之後，耐克才於本週上架補庫球衣，上架初期還打出17%的折扣；不過到週四，耐克官網就已經撤下了該優惠。 缺貨問題不只是出在美國隊球衣上。耐克一共贊助16支國家隊，其中至少4支隊伍的球衣全部賣斷。巴西隊部分球衣在小組賽階段就已售空，法國隊正版球員版球衣更是在淘汰賽開始前便無貨可售。古根海姆證券零售業分析師西緬·西格爾認為，球衣斷貨只是耐克整體經營現狀的縮影。這類本該全價熱銷創收卻錯失視窗期的問題，根源在於公司內部多條業務線仍在不斷處理各類經營漏洞、被動補救問題。

FTSE 100 Edges Higher, Outperforming European Peers — London's FTSE 100 erased early losses to trade slightly higher at around 10,590 on Friday, hovering near its highest level since July 8 and putting the index on track for a weekly gain of roughly 0.9%. Unlike many major indices, the FTSE 100 has relatively little exposure to technology stocks and is instead heavily weighted toward defensive consumer and healthcare companies, sectors that appear to be in demand today.
